HEAD at `J434` — **T1: shadowtraffic-e2e flake fixed (root cause = read-before-flush, not weakened).** Both `TestTriadComposesEndToEnd` AND its sibling `TestRecordReplayRoundTripsCleanly` read the record binary's JSONL immediately after SIGTERM; under full-suite parallel load the record subprocess could be force-killed before its async drain flushed → "0 log entries" / short replay. Root fix: the drain flushes every `-flush-interval` (50ms) so entries are reader-visible WITHOUT graceful shutdown — NEW `waitForJSONLEntries` polls the log for the expected count (10s budget) BEFORE stopAndWait, decoupling the assertion from shutdown timing. Assertions unchanged (still want N entries). Verified: e2e 8× + full `go test ./...` 3× consecutive, all green. Dev-tooling only. Hard gates clean.

HEAD at `J408` — **Log subsystem slice 3/4: fan-out logger + open RoleLog DB + attach the sink (logging-to-DB is now LIVE).** NEW `internal/instrumentation/fanout.go`: `Fanout` slog.Handler dispatching each record (cloned) to every sub-handler, with runtime `Attach` (copy-on-write) — mirrors C# `DatabaseTarget.Register` adding the DB target to the running LogManager after the log DB is available. `NewLogger` now returns `(*slog.Logger, *Fanout)`. `cmd/sonarr/main.go`: `serve` gains the fanout; after the main DB it opens the RoleLog DB (NEW `buildLogDatastoreConfig` — SQLite always; Postgres only when `PostgresLogDb` set, else log DB disabled = C# LogDbEnabled=false), migrates it, builds `logs.Repo` + `logs.NewSink(...,Info)`, attaches the sink to the fanout, and defers sink+DB close. `logRepo` kept in scope for the next slice's GET /log + ClearLog wiring. Tests: fanout dispatch-to-all / attach-adds-handler (pre-attach handler sees both, new one only post-attach) / Enabled-any / per-handler level gate / WithAttrs-still-dispatches. Hard gates clean (build, race tests instrumentation + logs + cmd/sonarr + contract, vet, gofmt).

HEAD at `J407` — **Log subsystem slice 2/4: async slog→DB sink (the C# DatabaseTarget equivalent).** NEW `internal/logs/sink.go`: `Sink` is an `slog.Handler` that persists records into the log DB via `logs.Repo.Insert` on a background goroutine draining a buffered channel — never blocks the caller (full buffer → drop + counter), logs Info+ by default (matches the C# `LoggingRule("*", LogLevel.Info)`). Field mapping mirrors `DatabaseTarget.Write`: Time/Level/Message from the record; Logger from a `component`/`logger` attr; Exception/ExceptionType from an `err`/`error` attr, folded into the message. Standard shared-core handler pattern (`sinkCore` holds the one writer goroutine + channel; `WithAttrs` returns a light handler sharing it; `WithGroup` no-op for the flat fields). `Close` flushes the buffer (shutdown drain). NOT yet attached to the logger — that's slice 3 (fan-out + NewLogger + open RoleLog DB in main.go + attach), then slice 4 wires GET /log read + ClearLog→Purge. Tests: Enabled gate (Debug off/Info+on); handle persists w/ mapped fields; exception mapping + message fold; WithAttrs→Logger; Close flushes all 20 buffered; Close idempotent. Hard gates clean (build, race tests logs, vet, gofmt).

NEW `internal/logs` package: `Log` row type (Id/Message/Time/Logger/Exception/ExceptionType/Level — Method dropped at log-DB migration 064, matching C#) + `Repo` with `Insert` (RETURNING "Id", both dialects), `Paged` (whitelisted sort + total count), `Purge` (DELETE all), `Trim` (>7d cutoff, injected now). HEAD at `J401` — **Phase-4 auto-redownload-on-failure (RedownloadFailedDownloadService).** Closes the failure loop: J400 publishes `DownloadFailedEvent`; J401 re-searches for a replacement. NEW package `internal/redownload` ports `RedownloadFailedDownloadService` (`src/NzbDrone.Core/Download/RedownloadFailedDownloadService.cs`) as the `IHandle<DownloadFailedEvent>`. Decision parity: SkipRedownload → skip; `AutoRedownloadFailed` disabled → skip; `ReleaseSource==InteractiveSearch && !AutoRedownloadFailedFromInteractiveSearch` → skip; one episode → episode search; failed-set == whole season → season search; else (double-episode) → episode search of the set. Narrow consumer interfaces (`Config`/`EpisodeLookup`/`EpisodeSearcher`/`SeasonSearcher`) — same search-dispatch indirection `episoderefreshed` uses (J209/J210); `EpisodeSearcher` reuses the existing `episodeRefreshedSearcherAdapter` (loops ids → `indexersearch.Service.EpisodeSearch`), `SeasonSearcher` is a new `redownloadSeasonSearchAdapter` (→ `SeasonSearch(...,missingOnly=false)`). Subscribed in main.go AFTER the history + blocklist DownloadFailed handlers to mirror C# `[EventHandleOrder.Last]`. NOTE: the search→grab (ProcessDecisions/DownloadReport) step is itself a pre-existing project-wide gap — `indexersearch.Service.EpisodeSearch/SeasonSearch` return evaluated decisions + fire real indexer queries + stamp LastSearchTime but don't yet auto-grab the best; this slice is exactly as functional as every other search trigger (MissingEpisodeSearch, episoderefreshed), so it's faithful, not a no-op. Tests: skip/auto-disabled/interactive-disabled skip; interactive-enabled + single + whole-season + partial-double dispatch; empty-ids no-op; lookup-error propagates; nil-ctor. Hard gates clean (build, race tests redownload + downloadprocessing + cmd/sonarr + contract, vet, gofmt).

HEAD at `J400` — **Phase-4 download-failure arm (FailedDownloadService.Check + ProcessFailed).** Continues the lifecycle: J399 auto-imports completed downloads; J400 auto-fails the failed ones. Ports `FailedDownloadService.Check`+`ProcessFailed` (`src/NzbDrone.Core/Download/FailedDownloadService.cs:85-138`) into `downloadprocessing.Service.ProcessTrackedDownloads`: a tracked download still `StateDownloading`/`StateImportBlocked` whose client `Item.Status==Failed` (or `IsEncrypted`) and which matched a series is marked `StateFailed` and publishes a `trackeddownloads.DownloadFailedEvent` — the same event the queue-delete blocklist arm publishes (J396), feeding the already-subscribed blocklist + history handlers. Failure-message precedence mirrors C# (`encrypted`→"Encrypted download detected"; failed-with-message→client message; else "Failed download detected"); `SkipRedownload=false` so a future redownload consumer would re-search. New optional `FailurePublisher` + `SetFailurePublisher` (arm no-ops when unwired); new `cmd/sonarr/downloadprocessing_failure_wiring.go` adapter over the bus, wired in main.go. C#'s two-phase Downloading→FailedPending→Failed is collapsed into one pass (consistent with J399's completed Check+Import collapse — same end state, event one cycle earlier). SAFE: no clients → empty cache → no-op (contract goldens unaffected); publish-error leaves Downloading for retry (no silent transition). Unmatched (no series) → warn + skip (C# "wasn't grabbed by Sonarr"). DEFERRED (documented): the redownload-on-failure search consumer; rejection/partial-import handling; FailedPending grace-cycle. Tests: failed-matched publishes+marks; encrypted message; unmatched skip; unwired no-op; publish-error not-marked (+ J399 import tests still green). Hard gates clean: build, `go test -race ./internal/downloadprocessing/... ./internal/downloadmonitoring/... ./internal/commands/... ./cmd/sonarr/... ./test/contract/...`, vet, gofmt.

HEAD at `J399` — **Phase-4 capstone: automatic import of completed downloads (ProcessMonitoredDownloads).** The download loop is now end-to-end: grab → J394 populates the queue from live clients → J399 auto-imports completed ones. Ports `DownloadProcessingService.Execute(ProcessMonitoredDownloadsCommand)` → `CompletedDownloadService.Import` (happy path). **NEW `internal/downloadprocessing/service.go`**: `Service.ProcessTrackedDownloads` walks the tracked-download cache; for each download still `StateDownloading` whose client `Item.Status==Completed` and which matched a series (`RemoteEpisode.Series`), hands the output path to the existing `downloadedimport.Service.ProcessPath` (the same primitive ManualImport uses) with the series + item; when every result imported, marks the tracked download `StateImported`. **`internal/downloadmonitoring`**: optional `MonitoredDownloadProcessor` + `SetProcessor`; `Refresh` runs it after populating the cache, so auto-import happens on every 1-min poll cycle. **NEW `internal/commands/handlers/processmonitoreddownloads.go`**: command handler (manual/API trigger). Wired in main.go from trackedDownloadsSvc + dimpSvc. SAFE: no clients → empty cache → processor no-ops (contract goldens unaffected, verified). DEFERRED (documented, NOT silently skipped): rejection/partial-import state machine (left Downloading for retry), script-import/ValidatePath/remove-completed-download cleanup/DownloadCompletedEvent, import-blocked state for unmatched downloads. Tests: downloadprocessing (completed-matched imports+marks; not-completed/unmatched/already-imported skip; partial not marked) + downloadmonitoring (Refresh runs processor) + handler dispatch/nil-ctor. Hard gates clean: build, `go test -race ./internal/downloadprocessing/... ./internal/downloadmonitoring/... ./internal/commands/... ./cmd/sonarr/... ./test/contract/...`, vet, gofmt. The grab→download→import loop's happy path now works end-to-end. Remaining: auto-redownload-on-failure consumer, the rejection/failure state machine, lower-pri admin commands (ResetApiKey/ResetQualityDefinitions/ClearLog), #8 cosmetic, #9 provider-gated.

HEAD at `J395` — **Phase-4 unblock, #5 part 2: queue-delete removeFromClient + changeCategory.** Now that J394 populates the queue from live clients, the queue-delete side-effects act on real tracked downloads. **`internal/api/v3/queue_write.go`**: new `QueueRemoveOptions` (the 4 flags; removeFromClient defaults TRUE per C#) + optional-extension `QueueItemRemover` interface + `ErrQueueDownloadClientUnavailable` sentinel (→400). Both single + bulk DELETE handlers parse the query flags and delegate to the remover when the writer implements it (else the pre-J395 cache-only Remove); single maps not-found→404 + sentinel→400, bulk logs-and-continues per id (C# RemoveMany posture). **NEW `cmd/sonarr/queue_remove_wiring.go`**: `queueRemoveAdapter` (embeds `*queue.Service` → still satisfies `apiv3.QueueWriter`) ports C# `QueueController.Remove(trackedDownload,...)` — resolve the queue row's tracked download → removeFromClient `client.RemoveItem(item,true)` / else changeCategory `client.MarkItemAsImported` → `StopTracking` → cache `Remove`. Live client resolved via injected `queueClientResolver` (production `downloadClientResolver` scans rows + `registry.Construct`; behind an interface for testability). Threaded through buildHostServer→v3Routes (one new `apiv3.QueueWriter` param), wired to v3 + v5 queue DELETE/{id}+bulk routes. blocklist arm = documented no-op (J396). Tests: handler (remover delegation, opt parsing incl. removeFromClient=false override, 404/400 mapping, bulk) + cmd/sonarr adapter (removeFromClient→RemoveItem; changeCategory→MarkItemAsImported; resolver-fail→sentinel; no-flags still stops/removes; unknown id→false). A nil model → nil return so the caller's omitempty pointer drops the key. **`internal/api/v3/episodefile.go`**: `ToEpisodeFileResource` assigns `MediaInfo: ToMediaInfoResource(f.MediaInfo, f.SceneName)`. Empty-DB contract preserved (zero rows; nil-MediaInfo fixtures drop the key). 1 new test (`TestEpisodeFile_MediaInfoProjection_J374` — a 2-audio-stream + 1-subtitle model asserts the flat summary, the shared video/runtime fields incl. the 23.976 fps round + `22:00` runtime, the typed stream lists incl. per-stream AC3 codec, the `1:05:09` hours-form runtime, and the nil→absent path). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/episodefile/... ./cmd/sonarr/... ./test/contract/...`; `go vet` + gofmt clean. Remaining EpisodeFileResource deferral: only **qualityCutoffNotMet** (needs the upgradable-spec service — same DEFERRED path as HistoryResource.QualityCutoffNotMet per J312; a mutation/service-dependent field rather than a pure projection, so likely the natural arc boundary). After that, the arc moves to the queue/history/blocklist embedder include-branches.

HEAD at `J372` — **Post-J365 resource-projection arc, slice 7: EpisodeResource.series (the includeSeries branch) — COMPLETES EpisodeResource.** Lands the last EpisodeResource embed. C# `EpisodeControllerWithSignalR.MapToResource` (`src/Sonarr.Api.V3/Episodes/EpisodeControllerWithSignalR.cs:61-118`) sets `resource.Series = series.ToResource()` — the **BASE** series projection with NO `MapCoversToLocal` and NO `LinkStatistics` (the controller applies those only to the top-level series GET, never to the embed); the list path keeps a per-request `seriesDict` keyed by seriesId so repeated ids resolve once. **`internal/api/v3/episode.go`**: `EpisodeResource` gains `Series *SeriesResource json:"series,omitempty"` (J302 pointer 3-state — nil→key absent / populated→embed); new optional-extension interface `EpisodeSeriesLookup interface { GetSeries(ctx, id) (*series.Series, error) }` (the J339/J342/J367/J369 type-assertion idiom); new `applyEpisodeSeries(ctx, res, seriesID, lister, includeSeries, cache)` helper — gates on the param + type-asserts the lister/getter, projects the looked-up series via the BASE `apiv3.ToSeriesResource` (NOT the enriched series handler shape — the image/stats populators are applied separately by the series handlers, never inside ToSeriesResource, so the embed is base-only by construction), and dedupes via an optional per-request `map[int]*SeriesResource` cache (mirrors C#'s seriesDict). `includeSeries` query param wired into all three episode handlers: EpisodeListHandler + EpisodeMonitorHandler allocate a shared cache before the row loop; EpisodeByIDHandler passes nil (single row). **`cmd/sonarr/host_wiring.go`**: the J342 `episodesWithEpisodeFileLookup` composite gains a `seriesSvc historySeriesLookup` field + a `GetSeries` forwarding method (satisfies `apiv3.EpisodeSeriesLookup`); the construction site sets `seriesSvc: seriesSvc`. That propagates the embed to every v3 episode wire site (`/api/v3/episode`, `/episode/{id}`, `/calendar/{id}`, `/wanted/{cutoff,missing}/{id}`) + the v5 episode/calendar/wanted aliases. Test fixtures don't implement the lookup so the empty fallback fires — the embed stays absent (omitempty). Empty-DB contract preserved: episode endpoints emit `[]` (zero rows); embedders reuse the unchanged scalar `ToEpisodeResource` so they're unaffected; `test/contract/replayer` + `hostbootstrap` pass. 3 new tests (`TestEpisodeSeries_J372` — absent without param / absent when lister lacks the lookup / populated base embed asserting series.id+title AND no `statistics` key to prove base-only; `TestEpisodeSeries_PerRequestCache_J372` — one GetSeries call across 3 same-series rows). HEAD at `J371` — **Post-J365 resource-projection arc, slice 6: EpisodeResource.images (the includeImages branch) — arc advances to EpisodeResource.** SeriesResource completed at J370; the arc's #2 priority is EpisodeResource. Wired the `includeImages` query param into all three v3 episode handlers — `EpisodeListHandler`, `EpisodeByIDHandler`, `EpisodeMonitorHandler` (the C# `SetEpisodesMonitored` also honours includeImages on its reloaded rows). v5 gets it automatically (v5 episode List/ByID alias the v3 handlers; Monitor wraps with the 202→200 demote). Two stale handler doc-comments updated (the includeImages-deferred notes). Empty-DB contract preserved: episode endpoints emit `[]` (zero rows); the embedders (queue/wanted/calendar/history) reuse `ToEpisodeResource` which is unchanged — they don't call applyEpisodeImages, so they're unaffected; `test/contract/replayer` + `hostbootstrap` pass. 2 new tests (`TestEpisodeImages_J371` — absent without the param / screenshot-coverType + persisted-url passthrough when includeImages=true; `TestEpisodeImages_EmptyList_J371` — `"images":[]` for the includeImages=true + no-images case). HEAD at `J370` — **Post-J365 resource-projection arc, slice 5: v5-additive statistics widening (monitoredEpisodeCount + releaseTypes + episodeFileQualities) — COMPLETES SeriesResource.** The micro-slice deferred from J369: the three statistics fields the C# v5 SeriesStatisticsResource/SeasonStatisticsResource carry but the v3 ones don't. The J368 seriesstats core already computes the underlying values (MonitoredEpisodeCount / ReleaseTypes []int / EpisodeFileQualities []int); this slice adds the wire mapping. **`internal/api/v3/series.go`**: both `SeriesStatisticsResource` + `SeasonStatisticsResource` gain `MonitoredEpisodeCount int json:"monitoredEpisodeCount,omitempty"`, `ReleaseTypes []string json:"releaseTypes,omitempty"`, `EpisodeFileQualities []QualityValue json:"episodeFileQualities,omitempty"` — emitted additively on the shared v3 type per the J318/J324 additive-divergence posture (v5 lights them up on populated rows; v3 clients ignore the extra keys; zero/empty collapses to absent via omitempty — the accepted v5-drops-zero trade-off). `ToSeriesStatisticsResource` + `ToSeasonStatisticsResource` populate them via two new helpers: `releaseTypeNames([]int)` maps the parser ReleaseType ints to the camelCase enum strings the C# JsonStringEnumConverter(CamelCase) emits ("singleEpisode"/"multiEpisode"/"seasonPack"/"unknown"); `toQualityValues([]int)` resolves each distinct quality id via `quality.FindByID` (unknown → Quality.Unknown, matching C# Quality.FindById) into the existing `QualityValue{id,name,source,resolution}` DTO (reusing J325's `QualitySourceName` camelCase source mapping). Documented faithful-degraded divergence: the C# SortQualities orders EpisodeFileQualities by quality-profile index; the Go port keeps the seriesstats core's ascending-id order (identical SET — the list order is cosmetic for the UI's quality badges — and threading the profile would re-introduce the qualityprofiles dep J369 dropped). No wiring change — the J369 `seriesReadEnricher.PopulateSeriesStats` → `LinkSeriesStatistics` path already carries these fields once the mappers populate them; both v3 + v5 surfaces get them (v3 additively). Empty-DB contract preserved: series.list goldens are `[]` (zero rows); `test/contract/replayer` + `hostbootstrap` pass. 1 new test (`TestToSeriesStatisticsResource_V5Additive_J370`: monitoredEpisodeCount=3 + releaseTypes=[singleEpisode,seasonPack] + episodeFileQualities resolved DTO with id/name/source + marshal-presence; AND the empty-stats omitempty drop of all three additive keys while the base v3 shape — seasonCount/releaseGroups[]/percentOfEpisodes — stays intact). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/series/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` (v3 1.76s, v5/series 2.86s, cmd/sonarr 4.52s); `go vet` + gofmt + contract clean. **SeriesResource is COMPLETE** — every C# SeriesResourceMapper field + every SeriesController-linked field is now projected on both surfaces (scalars, model-intrinsic band J366, images J367, statistics J368/J369, v5-additive statistics J370). The only SeriesResource gaps left are genuinely unported subsystems, each tracked elsewhere: seasons[].images (the includeSeasonImages query flag + season MediaCover rewrite), alternateTitles (the scene-mapping service, J329-pinned), isExcluded (the importlistexclusion service join). The arc now advances to **EpisodeResource** — the ~30-field projection across `internal/api/v3/episode.go` (DEFERRED: the joined Series/EpisodeFile/Images projection + includeImages) and the queue/wanted/calendar/history embedders that reuse it.

HEAD at `J369` — **Post-J365 resource-projection arc, slice 4: wire the statistics core into SeriesResource (statistics + seasons[].statistics + nextAiring/previousAiring + lastAired fallback).** Consumes the J368 `internal/seriesstats` core — the wire half of the core-then-wire split. **`internal/api/v3/series.go`** (imports `seriesstats`): new `SeriesStatisticsResource{seasonCount, episodeFileCount, episodeCount, totalEpisodeCount, sizeOnDisk, releaseGroups, percentOfEpisodes}` + `SeasonStatisticsResource{nextAiring?, previousAiring?, episodeFileCount, episodeCount, totalEpisodeCount, sizeOnDisk, releaseGroups, percentOfEpisodes}` (the shape shared by C# v3+v5; releaseGroups always-emit `[]`; percentOfEpisodes the C# computed property = `0` when episodeCount==0 else `fileCount/episodeCount*100`). SeriesResource gains `Statistics *SeriesStatisticsResource`, `NextAiring *time.Time`, `PreviousAiring *time.Time` (all omitempty); SeasonResource gains `Statistics *SeasonStatisticsResource omitempty`. New `ToSeriesStatisticsResource(st, seasons)` (SeasonCount = count of resource seasons with SeasonNumber>0 — C# `seasons.Count(s => s.SeasonNumber > 0)`) + `ToSeasonStatisticsResource(st)` mappers, and the pure `LinkSeriesStatistics(res, st)` mirroring C# `SeriesController.LinkSeriesStatistics`: `LastAired ??= st.LastAired` (fill only when the row has none), `PreviousAiring`/`NextAiring` = st values, `Statistics` set, each `Seasons[i].Statistics` matched from `st.SeasonStatistics` by SeasonNumber (unmatched seasons stay nil). Wired via the J339/J345/J367 optional-extension idiom: `SeriesStatsPopulator interface { PopulateSeriesStats(ctx, res) }` + `ApplySeriesStatsPopulator(ctx, res, svc any)` (ctx-threaded — unlike the sync image populator — since stats reads hit the DB), called by the List + ByID handlers per resource (before the image populator). **`cmd/sonarr/series_image_wiring.go`**: the J367 `seriesImagePopulator` is generalised to `seriesReadEnricher{*series.Service; mc *mediacover.Service; eps seriesStatsEpisodeLister; files seriesStatsFileLister}` — ONE value now implements BOTH `SeriesImagePopulator` and `SeriesStatsPopulator` (the handlers pass a single lister/getter through both Apply helpers). `PopulateSeriesStats` loads the series' episodes (`episodes.Repo.AllBySeriesID`) + files (`episodefiles.Service.GetFilesBySeries`), runs `seriesstats.Compute(eps, files, time.Now().UTC())`, and delegates to `apiv3.LinkSeriesStatistics`; a load error degrades to nil statistics rather than failing the request. Per-series loading (N+1 on the list path; single series on the hot by-id detail path) — the established aggregate-in-Go posture. `host_wiring.go` updates the `seriesImgSvc` construction to the enricher (reuses the in-scope `episodesRepo` + `episodeFilesSvc` — no new buildHostServer params); the 4 GET sites already route through it (v3+v5 list/by-id), so v5 gets statistics automatically. **v5-additive statistics fields DEFERRED** to a follow-up widening: `monitoredEpisodeCount`, `releaseTypes` (enum-name strings), `episodeFileQualities` (Quality DTOs) — the seriesstats core already computes the underlying values; only the release-type-enum + quality-DTO wire mapping remains (J370). Empty-DB contract preserved: series.list goldens are `[]` (zero rows) → statistics unobservable; `test/contract/replayer` + `hostbootstrap` pass. 3 new tests (`TestLinkSeriesStatistics_J369` — seasonCount-from-seasons>0 + percentOfEpisodes=50 + per-season match-by-number incl. an unmatched-stays-nil season + airing + lastAired-fallback-fills; `..._LastAiredPreserved_J369` — existing row LastAired NOT overwritten; `TestSeriesListHandler_StatsPopulator_J369` — populator fills statistics / plain lister leaves nil). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/series/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` (v3 2.71s, v5/series 1.67s, cmd/sonarr 4.29s); `go vet` + gofmt + contract clean. **SeriesResource is now effectively complete** — every C#-mapper + controller-linked field is projected (scalars, model-intrinsic band J366, images J367, statistics J368/J369). Remaining deferrals are distinct unported-subsystem gates: the v5-additive statistics trio (J370 micro-slice), seasons[].images (includeSeasonImages flag), alternateTitles (scene-mapping service), isExcluded (importlistexclusion join). The arc next advances to EpisodeResource (the ~30-field projection across episode.go + the queue/wanted/calendar/history embedders).

HEAD at `J368` — **Post-J365 resource-projection arc, slice 3: SeriesResource.statistics CORE — the episode/episodefile aggregation (`internal/seriesstats`).** The biggest remaining SeriesResource piece, and the one that unblocks several deferred fields at once (statistics + seasons[].statistics + nextAiring/previousAiring + the lastAired fallback). Split core-then-wire (the J360→J361 cutoff-unmet precedent): this slice lands the correctness-critical aggregation; J369 wires it into the projection via a populator. **New `internal/seriesstats` package** ports `NzbDrone.Core.SeriesStats` (SeriesStatistics + SeasonStatistics models + the SeriesStatisticsService/Repository aggregation). C# runs two grouped SQL queries (Episodes grouped by series+season for counts/airing; EpisodeFiles grouped for size/release-groups/types/qualities) merged per (seriesId, seasonNumber); the Go port computes the IDENTICAL aggregation in memory via the pure `Compute(eps []episodes.Episode, files []episodefiles.File, now time.Time) SeriesStatistics` — same "aggregate-in-Go, faithful-degraded" posture as the wanted/cutoff paths, keeping the counting semantics in one exhaustively-testable function (the numbers match the C# CASE expressions exactly; only SQL-GROUP-BY vs in-memory differs). Per-season counting mirrors `SeriesStatisticsRepository.EpisodesBuilder`: TotalEpisodeCount=COUNT(*); AvailableEpisodeCount=COUNT(airedByNow OR hasFile); EpisodeCount=COUNT((monitored AND airedByNow) OR hasFile); EpisodeFileCount=COUNT(hasFile); MonitoredEpisodeCount=COUNT(monitored); NextAiring=MIN(AirDateUtc) over monitored eps with AirDateUtc>=now; PreviousAiring=MAX(AirDateUtc) over monitored eps with AirDateUtc<now; LastAired=MAX(AirDate date-only column) — where airedByNow = AirDateUtc!=nil && <=now, hasFile = EpisodeFileID!=nil && >0. File aggregation per season: SizeOnDisk=SUM(Size); distinct non-empty ReleaseGroups (sorted); distinct ReleaseTypes; distinct quality ids parsed from the persisted `{"quality":<id>,...}` JSON (the canonical quality.Model.MarshalJSON bare-int shape — confirmed against quality/model.go:78 + the J360 cutoff LIKE; the nested `{"quality":{"id":...}}` test fixture is legacy). Series rollup: sums + distinct-unions; NextAiring=min-of-seasons; PreviousAiring=max-of-seasons; LastAired=max-of-seasons WHERE SeasonNumber>0 (the C# specials exclusion); SeasonStatistics sorted by SeasonNumber. Qualities are sorted ascending in the core for determinism — the API tier re-sorts by quality profile (C# `SortQualities`) at wire time. The full model carries the v5-additive fields too (MonitoredEpisodeCount, ReleaseTypes, EpisodeFileQualities, AvailableEpisodeCount) so both the v3 + v5 projection slices consume one core. 6 unit tests (`internal/seriesstats/seriesstats_test.go`): counts (total/available/episode/file/monitored across a mixed season + a specials season); airing (min-future-monitored / max-past-monitored / max-AirDate, with an unmonitored-future episode proving it's excluded); lastAired-excludes-season0; file-aggregation (distinct sorted groups + types + qualities unioned across seasons, empty group/quality skipped); empty input (zero + non-nil slices); nil-AirDateUtc (file-bearing episode counts but doesn't set airing windows). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/seriesstats/...` (1.66s); `go vet` + gofmt clean. No API wire-up yet — J369 adds the `seriesStatsPopulator` (mirrors the J367 image populator + C# `SeriesController.LinkSeriesStatistics`): a per-request-cached series→stats lookup that fills SeriesResource.Statistics + each seasons[].Statistics + nextAiring/previousAiring + the `LastAired ??=` fallback, with the v3 SeriesStatisticsResource/SeasonStatisticsResource wire types (counts + sizeOnDisk + releaseGroups + percentOfEpisodes + seasonCount) and the v5-additive widening. After J369, SeriesResource is effectively complete (only seasons[].images / alternateTitles / isExcluded remain — each a distinct unported-subsystem gate) and the arc advances to EpisodeResource.

HEAD at `J367` — **Post-J365 resource-projection arc, slice 2: SeriesResource.images — MediaCover local-URL rewrite via a handler-tier populator.** The biggest remaining single SeriesResource field, and the first service-join projection of the arc (every model-intrinsic field landed at J366). C# emits `images` as a non-null `List<MediaCover>` from `model.Images.JsonClone()` in the mapper, but rewrites each entry's `Url` to the local `<urlBase>/MediaCover/<seriesId>/<type>.<ext>?lastWrite=<ticks>` form at the CONTROLLER layer (`SeriesController.MapCoversToLocal` → `_coverMapper.ConvertToLocalUrls`, NOT in `ToResource`) — the persisted `Url` is empty/stale. The Go port mirrors that split exactly. **`internal/api/v3/series.go`**: new `MediaCoverResource{coverType string, url string, remoteUrl string omitempty}` (coverType is the camelCase enum string; openapi has url+remoteUrl nullable → url always-emit, remoteUrl omitempty); `SeriesResource.Images []MediaCoverResource json:"images"` (always-non-null array); `ToSeriesResource` decodes the persisted `Series.Images` blob via the existing `mediacover.DecodeSeriesImages` (which reads the Go-port frozen-int coverType ordinal) → wire shape with `CoverType.String()`, persisted url, remoteUrl (empty/invalid → `[]`). The URL rewrite is the J339/J345 optional-extension idiom: new `SeriesImagePopulator` interface + `ApplySeriesImagePopulator(res, svc any)` helper that the List + ByID handlers call per resource (type-asserts the lister/getter; rewrites `res.Images[].Url` in place when the impl satisfies it, else leaves the persisted Url — preserving test-fixture behaviour). **`cmd/sonarr/series_image_wiring.go`** (new): `seriesImagePopulator{*series.Service; mc *mediacover.Service}` embeds the service (drop-in for the read interfaces) and implements `PopulateSeriesImages` by rebuilding `[]mediacover.Cover` (via `mediacover.ParseCoverType` — unknown→CoverTypeUnknown which ConvertToLocalUrls skips) → `mc.ConvertToLocalUrls(seriesID, covers)` → copies the rewritten Url back; 3 compile-time interface guards. **`cmd/sonarr/host_wiring.go`**: `buildHostServer` + `v3Routes` gain a `*mediacover.Service` param (named `mediaCoverConvertSvc` to avoid colliding with the existing `mediaCoverLister`); a `seriesImgSvc` wrapper is built once and substituted ONLY at the 4 GET sites (v3 `/api/v3/series` + `/{id}`, v5 `/api/v5/series` + `/{id}`) — the write handlers keep the bare `seriesSvc`. **`main.go`** passes the real `mediaCoverSvc` (the J364 one, with fs+urlBase+resizer) into the call. v5 fires automatically (its ListHandler/ByIDHandler wrap the v3 handlers with the same lister; the languageProfileId-strip middleware runs after). Empty-DB contract preserved: series.list goldens are `[]` (zero rows), so `images:[]` is unobservable — `test/contract/replayer` + `hostbootstrap` both pass. 3 new tests (`..._ImagesProjection_J367` int-ordinal→string coverType + persisted-url + remoteUrl-omitempty + clearlogo mapping; `..._ImagesProjection_EmptyRow_J367` non-null `images:[]`; `TestSeriesListHandler_ImagePopulator_J367` with-populator rewrites Url / without-populator preserves persisted Url). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/series/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` (v3 1.78s, v5/series 2.69s, cmd/sonarr 4.35s); `go vet` + gofmt clean. Remaining SeriesResource deferrals (all need an episode/episodefile statistics join or an unported subsystem): statistics + seasons[].statistics + nextAiring/previousAiring/episodesChanged (the big next candidate — per-request-cached aggregation), seasons[].images (the includeSeasonImages flag), alternateTitles (scene-mapping), isExcluded (importlistexclusion join). After statistics, SeriesResource is effectively complete and the arc advances to EpisodeResource.

HEAD at `J366` — **Post-J365 resource-projection arc, slice 1: SeriesResource model-intrinsic projection (the densest deferral set, no service join).** First slice of the new arc (API resource-projection completeness, biggest/most-UI-consumed resource first). The C# `SeriesResourceMapper.ToResource` (`src/Sonarr.Api.V3/Series/SeriesResource.cs:80-145`) sets a band of fields straight off the persisted `model` with no cross-row service join; the Go port previously deferred all of them (`internal/api/v3/series.go` carried only the scalar passthrough subset). This slice populates that whole model-intrinsic band on the shared `apiv3.SeriesResource` (v5 variable-aliases it, so both surfaces light up; v5 still strips `languageProfileId`): **seasons** (`[]SeasonResource{seasonNumber,monitored}` decoded from the `Series.Seasons` opaque-JSON column — per-season statistics+images stay deferred; always-non-null `[]` matching the C# `model.Seasons.ToResource(...)`), **originalLanguage** (`LanguageRef{id,name}` via the existing `ToLanguageRef(s.OriginalLanguage)`; always emitted, id 0 → Unknown — C# non-nullable `Language`), **genres** (`[]string` decoded from `Series.Genres` JSON, replacing the hard-coded `[]`), **ratings** (`*RatingsResource{votes,value}` decoded from `Series.Ratings` JSON; nil/omitempty when the column is empty — C# Ratings is uninitialized so null drops the key), **added** (`time.Time`, always emitted, zero-time when unset — C# `DateTime` non-nullable, mirrors the episodefile `dateAdded` precedent), **firstAired/lastAired** (`*time.Time` omitempty — C# `DateTime?`), and **addOptions** (`*json.RawMessage` verbatim passthrough of the `Series.AddOptions` JSON column; nil/omitempty when empty — byte-faithful since the persisted bytes are already in the camelCase wire shape, avoids re-deriving the AddSeriesOptions DTO). New v3 types `SeasonResource{SeasonNumber,Monitored}` + `RatingsResource{Votes,Value}` + the `decodeSeriesGenres`/`decodeSeriesSeasons`/`decodeSeriesRatings`/`rawJSONOrNil` helpers. Empty-DB contract preserved: the recorded series.list goldens are `[]` (zero rows), so the populated-row additions are unobservable at the contract level (verified — the `test/contract/replayer` suite still passes; the `test/contract/hostbootstrap` package has a PRE-EXISTING build break unrelated to this slice — a queue test-fake `PagedQueue` signature drift from the J357/J358 queue-filter work). Still DEFERRED on SeriesResource (need a service join / unported subsystem): images + seasons[].images (MediaCover local-URL rewrite — next slice candidate), statistics + seasons[].statistics + nextAiring/previousAiring/episodesChanged (episode/episodefile statistics join), alternateTitles (scene-mapping, J329-pinned), isExcluded (importlistexclusion join), remotePoster/folder (lookup-flow / C#-unset). 2 new tests (`TestToSeriesResource_ModelIntrinsicProjection_J366` populated round-trip with marshal-contract asserts incl. season-images-must-not-leak; `..._EmptyRow_J366` pins `seasons:[]`/`genres:[]`/Unknown-language/zero-added + ratings/addOptions/firstAired/lastAired key-absence). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/series/...` (v3 1.94s, v5/series 2.07s); `go vet` + gofmt clean. Next slice candidate: SeriesResource.images (the MediaCover local-URL rewrite populator, wiring the mediacover service into the projection) — the biggest remaining single SeriesResource field.

HEAD at `J365` — **Post-J352 residual-arc follow-up: MediaCover resize tier (closes the last documented deferral).** User-directed continuation after the residual-arc completed at J364. Ports C# `MediaCover/ImageResizer.cs` (the SixLabors.ImageSharp `Resize(0, height)` wrapper) + `EnsureResizedCovers` (`MediaCoverService.cs:176-219`). **Dependency decision**: added `golang.org/x/image v0.41.0` (Go-team maintained, minimal transitive deps) for high-quality scaling — `draw.CatmullRom` (cubic kernel comparable to ImageSharp's default resampler), with stdlib `image/jpeg` (quality 92, matching C# `JpegEncoder{Quality=92}`) + `image/png` (alpha-preserving, for Clearlogo) for decode/encode. New `internal/mediacover/resize.go` `ImageResizer` (implements the new `Resizer` interface): decode → scale to target height with auto width (aspect preserved, rounded, clamped ≥1) → encode (png for `.png` dest, else jpeg q92) → atomic temp-`.part`+rename, with failure cleanup (removes partial + any stale dest, mirroring the C# catch-and-delete at `:209-218`). `Service` gains an optional `resizer` (via `SetResizer`); `EnsureCovers` restructured to the C# `(cover, alreadyExists)` two-pass shape — download pass, then a resize pass over ALL covers (`forceResize = !alreadyExists`), so already-present masters still get missing/zero-byte variants regenerated; new `ensureResizedCovers` ports `:176-219` (per `ResizeHeights` tier, skip when present+non-zero unless forced, resize failures logged+swallowed). nil resizer → download-only (the J363 posture). Wired in main.go: `mediaCoverSvc.SetResizer(mediacover.NewImageResizer())`. 8 new tests: 3 real-image round-trips (aspect+height preserved, png-for-clearlogo, missing-source-errors) + 5 wiring (resizes-fresh-download, skips-present-variant, regenerates-zero-byte, nil-resizer-skips, absorbs-resize-error). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/mediacover/ ./cmd/sonarr/...` (mediacover 1.46s, cmd/sonarr 3.70s); `go vet` + gofmt clean; `go mod tidy` clean (x/image direct + x/sys reclassified direct). **The MediaCover subsystem is now fully ported** (URL-rewrite 3.BO + download J363/J364 + resize J365) — no remaining MediaCover deferrals. A refreshed/added series now fetches its covers AND writes the poster-500/250, banner-70/35, fanart-360/180 (etc.) height variants the React UI requests.

HEAD at `J364` — **Post-J352 residual-arc, Arc 3 (smaller residuals) slice 6: wire the MediaCover cover-download subscriber (DRAINS Arc 3 + COMPLETES the Post-J352 residual-arc).** Consumes the J363 download surface. New `cmd/sonarr/mediacover_wiring.go` bridges the production services into the `internal/mediacover` narrow interfaces: `mediaCoverFS` (wraps `*disk.Provider` — adds `FileExists` via `os.Stat`, converts `FileGetLastWrite`'s `time.Time` → .NET ticks via the 621355968000000000 Unix-epoch offset), `mediaCoverFolders` (`<AppData>/MediaCover`), `mediaCoverURLBase` (configfile `UrlBase`), and `mediaCoverDownloader` (wraps `*httpclient.Client` — HEAD→`Headers.ContentLength()` + `DownloadFile`), each with a compile-time guard. main.go constructs the `mediacover.Service` after the httpClient block, binds the downloader via `SetDownloader`, and subscribes two bus arms (C# `MediaCoverService.HandleAsync`): `SeriesUpdatedEvent` → `HandleSeriesUpdated` (downloads covers on the post-RefreshSeries metadata sync, then republishes `CoversUpdatedEvent` with the `updated` flag for SignalR/UI consumers — mirrors C# `:237`), and `SeriesDeletedEvent` → `HandleSeriesDeleted` (wipes each deleted series' cover folder). Behavioural flip: a refreshed/added series now actually fetches its poster/banner/fanart/etc. to `<AppData>/MediaCover/<id>/`, and deleting a series cleans them up — previously the mediacover.Service was never instantiated in production. (Resize variants stay deferred per J363 — full-size download only; C# treats resize as best-effort.) Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/mediacover/ ./cmd/sonarr/...` (mediacover 1.41s, cmd/sonarr 3.69s); `go vet` + gofmt clean. **Arc 3 (smaller residuals) COMPLETE** — Queue CustomFormatScore recompute (J359) + the cutoff-unmet chain (J360-J362) + the MediaCover download subscriber (J363-J364). **All three Post-J352 residual arcs are drained** (Arc 1 ImportDecisionMaker spec pipeline J353-J356; Arc 2 Queue SORT+filters J357-J358; Arc 3 smaller residuals J359-J364). The only documented remaining deferral is the MediaCover ImageSharp resize tier, which needs a deliberate Go image-resize dependency decision (out of scope for a faithful-degraded port).

HEAD at `J363` — **Post-J352 residual-arc, Arc 3 (smaller residuals) slice 5: MediaCover cover-download surface (EnsureCovers / CoverAlreadyExists / DownloadCover / HandleSeriesUpdated).** User-directed continuation of the final Arc 3 item (the image-download subscriber off the series-mutation event). Orientation found the `internal/mediacover` package already had the local-disk + URL-rewrite surface (slice 3.BO) but had deferred the HTTP-gated download arm; the blocker (no `IHttpClient`) is now lifted — `httpclient.Client` (real impl with `Head` + `DownloadFile`) is wired in main.go. The C# subscriber is actually `IHandleAsync<SeriesUpdatedEvent>` + `<SeriesDeletedEvent>` (covers download on the post-add refresh, not `SeriesAddedEvent` directly). This slice lands the download surface; J364 wires the bus subscriber. New `CoverDownloader` optional dep (`ContentLength` HEAD-probe + `DownloadFile`), bound post-construction via `Service.SetDownloader` so the existing `NewService` signature + callers/tests stay stable; `Filesystem` gains `GetFileSize`. Ported: `coverAlreadyExists` (C# `CoverAlreadyExistsSpecification` — file exists AND size == remote Content-Length; any probe failure → re-download), `EnsureCovers` (C# `:110-166` download half — per cover, skip Unknown, download when missing/stale, per-cover errors absorbed+logged so one bad URL doesn't abort the series; returns the `updated` flag), `downloadCover`, and `HandleSeriesUpdated` (delegates to EnsureCovers, returns updated for the caller to publish `CoversUpdatedEvent`). The ImageSharp resize half stays deferred (no Go image-resize dep decision made — C# treats resize as best-effort with full-size fallback, so download-only is faithful-degraded). nil downloader → the whole arm no-ops (pre-J363 posture). 6 new tests (nil-downloader no-op / downloads-missing + skips-Unknown / skips-on-size-match / re-downloads-on-size-mismatch / absorbs-per-cover-download-error / HandleSeriesUpdated-delegates) with a fake downloader + extended stubFS. Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/mediacover/` (1.62s); `go vet` + gofmt clean. J364 constructs the Service in main.go, binds an httpClient-backed `CoverDownloader` adapter, and subscribes `HandleSeriesUpdated`→`SeriesUpdatedEvent` + `HandleSeriesDeleted`→`SeriesDeletedEvent` on the bus — completing the last Arc 3 residual.

HEAD at `J362` — **Post-J352 residual-arc, Arc 3 (smaller residuals) slice 4: CutoffUnmetEpisodeSearchCommand handler.** Now-unblocked by the J360/J361 cutoff-unmet enumerator. Bound in the `handlers.Deps` construction (`episodesRepo` + `qualityProfilesSvc` in scope). 4 handler unit tests (stub-when-unwired / dispatches-per-episode / seriesId-filter / lister-error-propagates), reusing the missing-handler's `stubEpisodeSearcher`. Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/commands/handlers/ ./cmd/sonarr/...` (handlers 41.3s, cmd/sonarr 3.92s); `go vet` + gofmt clean. The C# scope-deferrals (queued-episode subtraction; SearchForBulkEpisodes season-grouping) carry over identically from MissingEpisodeSearch. **Arc 3 nearly drained** — only the image-download subscriber off SeriesAddedEvent remains, and it needs a whole new HTTP-download MediaCover subsystem (no `internal/mediacover`; external I/O), which is out of scope for the autonomous loop's no-risky-external-actions posture.
