HEAD at `J361` — **Post-J352 residual-arc, Arc 3 (smaller residuals) slice 3: wire the cutoff-unmet enumerator into the wanted/cutoff endpoint (v3+v5).** Second flesh of the cutoff-unmet enumerator — consumes the J360 core. The `wantedRepoLister` (cmd/sonarr) gains a `profiles *qualityprofiles.Service` field; its `PagedWanted` cutoff branch — which since slice 4.DV returned an honest empty result ("the qualityprofile port hasn't landed yet") — now computes the per-profile below-cutoff quality-id sets via the new `belowCutoffByProfile` helper (runs `qualityprofiles.QualitiesBelowCutoff` over `qualityProfilesSvc.All`) and enumerates via `episodes.Repo.WantedCutoffUnmet(ctx, below, true)` (monitored=true default, matching the missing branch + the recorded-corpus posture). The result pages in memory + projects via `apiv3.ToEpisodeResource`, identical to the missing branch. All four `wantedRepoLister` construction sites (v3+v5, missing+cutoff) updated to pass `profiles: qualityProfilesSvc` (in scope at every site). Behavioural flip: `/api/v3/wanted/cutoff` + `/api/v5/wanted/cutoff` now return episodes whose on-disk file ranks below the series' quality-profile cutoff, instead of always `[]`. No new cmd/sonarr DB test (package main has no datastore-test infra; the wiring is thin glue over the J360-unit-tested `QualitiesBelowCutoff` + `WantedCutoffUnmet`, both with 2-engine coverage). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./cmd/sonarr/...` (3.82s); `go vet` + gofmt clean. Arc 3 remaining: the CutoffUnmetEpisodeSearchCommand handler (now unblocked — needs the enumerator + the established MissingEpisodeSearch handler pattern + indexersearch EpisodeSearch dispatch) and the image-download subscriber (deferred — needs a new HTTP-download MediaCover subsystem, external I/O).

HEAD at `J360` — **Post-J352 residual-arc, Arc 3 (smaller residuals) slice 2: cutoff-unmet enumerator core (qualityprofiles below-cutoff computation + episodes cutoff-unmet repo query).** First flesh of the cutoff-unmet enumerator — the meatiest remaining residual (it unblocks the empty `/api/v3/wanted/cutoff` endpoint AND the CutoffUnmetEpisodeSearchCommand handler, both of which the J359 orientation found were blocked on this unported query; the image-download subscriber, the other Arc 3 item, needs a whole new HTTP-download MediaCover subsystem and is deferred as external-I/O-heavy). This slice lands the two correctness-sensitive core functions, each independently tested; the wanted/cutoff + command-handler wiring follows in J361. **`qualityprofiles.QualitiesBelowCutoff(p)`** ports the per-profile half of C# `EpisodeCutoffService.EpisodesWhereCutoffUnmet` (`:26-50`): effective cutoff = `UpgradeAllowed ? Cutoff : FirststAllowedQuality().Id`; `cutoffIndex = GetIndex(cutoff)`; below-cutoff = the flattened quality IDs of `Items[:cutoffIndex]` (profile Items are worst→best, so the prefix is everything ranked below the cutoff; group items flatten via `GetQualities`). New `firstAllowedQuality` helper ports `QualityProfile.FirststAllowedQuality()` (first Allowed item's quality, or its first group member); returns empty (not a throw) when nothing is allowed / cutoff not found / cutoff is the lowest — so a malformed profile doesn't abort the sweep. **`episodes.Repo.WantedCutoffUnmet(ctx, belowByProfile, monitoredOnly)`** ports `EpisodeRepository.EpisodesWhereCutoffUnmet` (`:126-256`, includeSpecials=false → SeasonNumber>=1): joins Episodes+Series+EpisodeFiles, keeps rows where the episode has a file AND `(Series.QualityProfileId = P AND EpisodeFiles.Quality LIKE '%"quality":Q,%')` for some below-cutoff (P,Q) — the LIKE pattern adjusted for the Go `quality.Model` JSON `{"quality":<id>,"revision":{...}}` (no colon-space; the trailing comma makes `"quality":Q,` an exact id-boundary match so id 2 never matches id 20, verified by the SQLite test). Empty belowByProfile short-circuits to empty (mirrors C# `qualitiesBelowCutoff.Empty()`). Full set returned for in-memory paging (the established WantedMissing posture). 7 qualityprofiles tests (UpgradeAllowed / lowest-cutoff-empty / upgrade-disabled-uses-first-allowed / group-cutoff / below-group-flattens / cutoff-not-found / no-allowed-edge) + a 2-engine (SQLite+Postgres) episodes repo suite (below-cutoff IN / above-cutoff OUT / special OUT / no-file OUT / unmonitored gated / empty-map short-circuit). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/qualityprofiles/ ./internal/episodes/` (qualityprofiles 32.4s, episodes 33.1s — the engine suites dominate); `go vet` + gofmt clean. J361 wires these into the `wantedRepoLister` cutoff branch (currently returns empty) for v3+v5, computing belowByProfile from `qualityProfilesSvc.All`; the CutoffUnmetEpisodeSearchCommand handler is a further follow-up (also needs indexersearch dispatch).

HEAD at `J359` — **Post-J352 residual-arc, Arc 3 (smaller residuals) slice 1: Queue CustomFormatScore recompute against the current quality profile.** Arc 2 complete, advancing to Arc 3. Orientation finding: of the three listed Arc 3 residuals, the CutoffUnmetEpisodeSearchCommand handler is NOT a small residual — it's blocked on the unported cutoff-unmet enumerator (`EpisodeCutoffService.EpisodesWhereCutoffUnmet` + `EpisodeRepository.EpisodesWhereCutoffUnmetBuilder`, a quality-below-cutoff computation + a multi-table SQL predicate with embedded-JSON quality LIKE matching, which also backs the currently-empty wanted/cutoff endpoint). That's a larger SQL-port sub-arc with real correctness stakes — deferred to its own slice. This tick takes the genuinely-small, clean residual: the Queue CustomFormatScore reconciliation. C# `QueueResource.cs:75` sets `CustomFormatScore = Series.QualityProfile.CalculateCustomFormatScore(model.RemoteEpisode.CustomFormats)` — i.e. RECOMPUTES against the CURRENT series quality profile; the Go J349 projection reused `q.RemoteEpisode.CustomFormatScore` (the decision-time precomputed score, which goes stale after a profile CF-score edit). The `queuePagedAdapter` gains a `qualityProfiles *qualityprofiles.Service` field; the PagedQueue projection loop now recomputes per row (when `q.Series != nil && q.RemoteEpisode != nil`) via `recomputeCustomFormatScore` — resolve `Series.QualityProfileID` → profile → `ParsedFormatItems` → present-set from the matched CustomFormats → `qualityprofiles.CalculateCustomFormatScore`. Any lookup failure (nil service, missing profile, parse error) keeps the precomputed score (defensive fallback — no regression vs J349). Wired at both v3 + v5 queue route sites (`qualityProfilesSvc` is a v3Routes param); the CustomFormats LIST projection is unchanged. 1 new adapter test pins the nil-QP fallback (Series set + no QP → precomputed 37 preserved); the positive recompute delegates to the already-tested `CalculateCustomFormatScore` + `ParsedFormatItems`. Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/queue/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` (v3 1.64s, v5/queue 2.03s, cmd/sonarr 4.34s); `go vet` + gofmt clean. Arc 3 remaining: the image-download subscriber off SeriesAddedEvent + the (larger) cutoff-unmet enumerator port (which would also enable the CutoffUnmetEpisodeSearchCommand handler + fix the empty wanted/cutoff endpoint).

HEAD at `J358` — **Post-J352 residual-arc, Arc 2 (Queue paging SORT + filter params) slice 2: Queue filter params (DRAINS Arc 2).** Ports the C# `QueueController.GetQueue` filter surface (`src/Sonarr.Api.V3/Queue/QueueController.cs:141,173-217`) the v3+v5 handlers previously parse-and-ignored: `seriesIds` / `protocol` / `languages` / `quality` / `status` + the default `includeUnknownSeriesItems=false` (which excludes series-less rows). New `internal/queue/filter.go` `FilterCriteria` + `FilterQueue(rows, criteria)`: drops `Series==nil` rows unless `IncludeUnknownSeriesItems`; then narrows by SeriesIDs (set) / Protocol (pointer) / Languages (any-of set) / Quality (Quality.Quality.ID set) / Status (set) — boolean-AND order matching C# `:191-214`; input not mutated. apiv3: new `QueueFilter` wire struct + `parseQueueFilter(r)` (repeated `int[]` params + single `protocol` + repeated `status` enum strings + `includeUnknownSeriesItems` bool, tolerating unparseable ints like the C# model binder); the `QueuePagedLister.PagedQueue` signature gains a `filter QueueFilter` param; both `QueueHandler` (v3) + `QueueHandlerV5` parse and pass it. cmd/sonarr `queuePagedAdapter.PagedQueue` now does `GetQueue → FilterQueue(criteria) → total=len(filtered) → SortQueue → paginate` (TotalRecords reflects the FILTERED count, matching C# `pagingSpec.TotalRecords = fullQueue.Count`); `queueFilterCriteria` maps the wire filter to the domain criteria, parsing the `protocol` string → `indexers.DownloadProtocol` and the `status` strings → `queue.QueueStatus` via case-insensitive enum-name match (unrecognised values drop to no-constraint, matching the C# nullable-enum binder). The 3 `PagedQueue` impls (apiv3 fake, v5 fake, adapter) + 9 adapter-test call sites updated to the new signature (adapter tests pass `IncludeUnknownSeriesItems: true` to preserve their projection/sort focus). 9 new domain tests (`internal/queue/filter_test.go`) + 2 handler param-parse tests (v3+v5) + 1 adapter wire→domain mapping test. Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/queue/... ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/queue/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` (queue 1.30s, v3 2.01s, v5/queue 1.64s, cmd/sonarr 3.57s); `go vet` + gofmt clean. **Arc 2 (Queue paging SORT + filter params) is COMPLETE** — the queue paged list now honours sort key + direction (J357) AND the full filter surface (J358) on both v3 + v5. The next tick advances to Arc 3 (smaller residuals: CutoffUnmetEpisodeSearchCommand handler; image-download subscriber off SeriesAddedEvent; Queue CustomFormatScore recompute-vs-precomputed reconciliation).

HEAD at `J357` — **Post-J352 residual-arc, Arc 2 (Queue paging SORT + filter params) slice 1: Queue paging SORT.** Arc 1 (ImportDecisionMaker spec pipeline) is substantively complete, so this tick advances to Arc 2. The Go queue paged adapter previously paginated in cache order, ignoring the `sortKey`/`sortDirection` the handler already parsed (the same DEFERRED posture the history/blocklist adapters carry). This slice ports the C# `QueueController.GetQueue` ordering (`src/Sonarr.Api.V3/Queue/QueueController.cs:219-321` — its MapToPagingSpec surface). New `internal/queue/sort.go` `SortQueue(rows, sortKey, ascending, qualityWeight)` orders the FULL queue before pagination across all ~18 sort keys: timeleft (default), estimatedCompletionTime, added, protocol, indexer, downloadClient, quality, languages/language, status, series.sortTitle, title, episode.airDateUtc/episodes.airDateUtc, episode.title/episodes.title, size, progress (+ the bare `episode`/unknown keys falling back to timeleft per C# `GetOrderByFunc` default). The three C# comparers are ported faithfully: `TimeleftComparer` + `DatetimeComparer` (nils/no-ETA sort LAST in ascending) and `LanguagesComparer` (empty last; multi-language piles after singles; single-vs-single by first language name). Every primary ordering is followed by the same unconditional secondary tiebreak C# applies — `ThenByDescending(progress)` where `progress = Size==0 ? 0 : 100 - SizeLeft/Size*100` (computed in float so it doesn't collapse to integer-division zero) — always descending regardless of primary direction. The "quality" key uses an injected `qualityWeight func(quality.Quality) int` (higher weight = higher quality, revision breaks ties) mirroring the C# `QualityModelComparer` built from the default profile; the `queuePagedAdapter` builds it from the `qualitydefinitions` catalogue (ordered by Weight ASC → quality.ID→weight map), falling back to Quality.ID ordering when unavailable. Adapter: `queuePagedAdapter` gains a `qualityDefs *qualitydefinitions.Service` field; `PagedQueue` now does `GetQueue → SortQueue(rows, req.SortKey, ascending, weightFunc) → paginate` (ascending only when SortDirection == "ascending"; default descending). Wired at both the v3 + v5 queue route sites (`qualityDefSvc` is a v3Routes param). 11 new domain tests (`internal/queue/sort_test.go` — title/size/timeleft-nils-last/added/progress-tiebreak/quality-weight/languages/episode-airdate/default-fallback/no-mutation). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/queue/... ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/queue/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` (queue 1.29s, v3 1.61s, v5/queue 2.02s, cmd/sonarr 4.64s); `go vet` + gofmt clean. Arc 2 remaining: the filter params (seriesIds / protocol / languages / quality / status) the v3+v5 QueueHandler currently parse-and-ignore — needs new query-param plumbing through the QueuePagedLister interface (the next Arc 2 slice).

HEAD at `J356` — **Post-J352 residual-arc, Arc 1 (ImportDecisionMaker spec pipeline) slice 4: ManualImport reprocess episodeIds-branch GetDecision rejections (DRAINS the ManualImport Rejections sub-arc).** Completes the J346-deferred Rejections list: the C# `ManualImportService.ReprocessItem` episodeIds branch (`:157-200`) assembles a LocalEpisode, runs `_aggregationService.Augment` then `_importDecisionMaker.GetDecision`, and surfaces the decision's rejections. Ported via the optional-extension type-assertion idiom (J338/J345) so the reprocess handler's signature is UNCHANGED (no churn across 27 call sites): new `apiv3.ManualImportDecisionRejector` interface; `reprocessOne` captures the user's `originalEpisodeIDs` before clearing `item.EpisodeIDs`, and in the episodeIds branch type-asserts the existing `calc` dep — when it also implements the rejector, `item.Rejections` is populated from `ReprocessDecisionRejections`. New `cmd/sonarr/manual_import_decision_wiring.go` `manualImportDecisionAdapter`: resolves the series + full episodes by id (for EpisodeFileID/AirDateUtc the Upgrade/EpisodeTitle specs read), reads file size, reconstructs Quality (`qualityModelFromResource` via `quality.FindByID`) / Languages / ReleaseGroup / IndexerFlags / ReleaseType from the reprocessed item, runs the SAME production `AggregationService` the real download-import flow uses (so manual-import rejections stay CONSISTENT with that path — Augment failure → UnableToParse), re-applies the user values (C# `:191-198`), runs `ImportDecisionMaker.GetDecision`, and projects `decision.Rejections` → `[]ManualImportRejectionResource{Reason: r.Message, Type: r.Kind.String()}`. Wiring: `parsingServiceWithPopulator` gains a `decisionRejector apiv3.ManualImportDecisionRejector` field + a delegating method (so it's the type-assertion carrier without host_wiring importing episodeimport); the adapter is constructed in main.go (where importDecisionMaker + aggregationSvc are in scope) and threaded through `buildHostServer` → `v3Routes` (one new param each). The v5 manualimport route shares the same `parsingSvcWithPopulator`, so it's covered automatically. 2 new tests (`..._GetDecisionRejections_J356` — a fake calc implementing both interfaces surfaces a rejection + receives the original episodeIds; `..._NoRejectorWhenCalcLacksInterface_J356` — a CF-only calc leaves rejections `[]`, pinning the type-assertion negative path). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/manualimport/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` (v3 1.76s, v5/manualimport 2.19s, cmd/sonarr 4.82s); `go vet` + gofmt clean. **The ManualImport Rejections list is now fully ported** (NoEpisodes J355 + GetDecision J356); only the parsed==nil UnableToParse edge remains deferred (the early-return branch). With the 14 specs ported + 13 wired + both ManualImport consumer surfaces ported, Arc 1 (ImportDecisionMaker spec pipeline) is substantively complete — the next tick advances to Arc 2 (Queue paging SORT + filter params) unless a cleaner Arc 1 residual surfaces.

HEAD at `J355` — **Post-J352 residual-arc, Arc 1 (ImportDecisionMaker spec pipeline) slice 3: ManualImport reprocess — typed rejection resource shape + the deterministic NoEpisodes "Episodes not selected" rejection (first flesh of the J346-deferred Rejections list).** With the 14-spec set ported + 13 wired (J353/J354), the remaining Arc 1 consumer item is surfacing decision Rejections on the manual-import reprocess endpoint (C# `ManualImportService.ReprocessItem`). This slice lands the well-bounded, decision-engine-free portion. Three changes: (1) new `apiv3.ManualImportRejectionResource{Reason string, Type string}` typed shape (json `reason` + `type`) mirroring C# `ManualImportResource.cs:84-105` `ImportRejectionResource` — `Reason` carries the rejection *message* (C# `ToResource` maps `rejection.Message`→`reason`, NOT the categorical enum), `Type` is the camelCase RejectionType string (`"permanent"`/`"temporary"`, verified against v3 `openapi.json` `RejectionType: {enum:[permanent,temporary]}`); plus `manualImportRejectionType{Permanent,Temporary}` wire constants. (2) `ManualImportReprocessItem.Rejections` field retyped `[]any` → `[]ManualImportRejectionResource` (wire-compatible — still emits `[]` when empty; no existing test referenced the field). (3) `reprocessOne` now captures the user's INPUT selection (`hadEpisodeIDs` / `inputSeasonProvided`) before reprocessing overwrites `item.SeasonNumber` + clears `item.EpisodeIDs`, then fires the NoEpisodes arm (C# `ManualImportService.cs:206-230`) when `!hadEpisodeIDs && inputSeasonProvided`: emits a single `{reason:"Episodes not selected", type:"permanent"}` rejection + empties `item.Episodes` (the user picked a season but no episodes within it → reject rather than import the whole season; no decision engine needed). The episodeIds-driven GetDecision rejections (C# `:157-200`, which runs the full ImportDecisionMaker over an aggregated LocalEpisode) stay DEFERRED — surfacing them needs LocalEpisode assembly + threading the orchestrator into the reprocess handler (a larger follow-up flesh). The NoEpisodes arm lives in the parsed!=nil path (the normal case: a real file assigned to a season); the parsed==nil edge keeps its existing deferral. 2 new tests (`TestManualImportReprocessHandler_NoEpisodesRejection_J355` — season-no-episodes → typed permanent rejection + empty Episodes; `..._NoNoEpisodesRejectionWithEpisodeIDs_J355` — episodeIds branch → no NoEpisodes, rejections `[]`). All existing reprocess tests unaffected (none send an input seasonNumber without episodeIds). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/manualimport/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` (v3 1.64s, v5/manualimport 1.30s, cmd/sonarr 3.80s); `go vet` + gofmt clean. Remaining Arc 1: the episodeIds-branch GetDecision rejections (decision-maker-driven) + the parsed==nil UnableToParse rejection.

HEAD at `J354` — **Post-J352 residual-arc, Arc 1 (ImportDecisionMaker spec pipeline) slice 2: wire UpgradeSpecification into the production import spec pipeline.** `UpgradeSpecification` (C# `Specifications/UpgradeSpecification.cs:13-104`) was ported + unit-tested in `specs.go` (slice-5 era) but its four narrow-interface deps had no production bindings, so it was absent from `cmd/sonarr/main.go`'s `importDecisionSpecs` (J353 brought 12 of 14 specs to wired; this slice makes it 13). New `cmd/sonarr/import_upgrade_spec_wiring.go` adds three adapters: `upgradeProfileViewBySeriesAdapter` (wraps `*series.Service` + `*qualityprofiles.Service` — resolves seriesID → QualityProfileID → `decisionengine.NewProfileView`, mirroring the C# `Episode.Series.QualityProfile.Value` lazy-load at `:30`); `upgradeEpisodeFileResolverAdapter` (wraps `episodeFilesSvc.GetFile`, translating `episodefiles.ErrNotFound` → `(nil, nil)` to honour the spec's "row-not-found → continue" contract at C# `:34-38`); `upgradeFormatCalculatorAdapter` (wraps `*series.Service` + `*customformats.Service` — resolves the file's series from `file.SeriesID` then runs `customformats.ParseCustomFormatForFile`, since the episodeimport `CustomFormatCalculator` interface drops the series arg). The fourth dep, `UpgradeConfig` (`DownloadPropersAndRepacks()`), is satisfied directly by `*config.Provider`. Wired via `episodeimport.NewUpgradeSpecification(configProv, …)` placed AFTER the `qualityProfilesSvc` + `customFormatsSvc` construction block (those two deps aren't in scope at the spec-list literal earlier in main.go) and appended to `importDecisionSpecs` just before `NewImportDecisionMaker`. Behaviourally: the spec rejects `NotQualityUpgrade` / `NotRevisionUpgrade` / `NotCustomFormatUpgrade(AfterRename)` when a candidate doesn't improve on an existing on-disk EpisodeFile; fresh imports (every episode `EpisodeFileID` nil/0) skip every arm → Accept, so wiring it only affects upgrade scenarios (C#-faithful — C# registers all specs via IoC). Lookup errors translate to Accept + Warn-log (the spec's established defensive posture). No new cmd/sonarr unit tests (matching the J3.92-3.95 wiring-slice posture — the spec logic is already covered by `TestUpgradeSpecification` in `internal/episodeimport/specs_test.go`; the gate is build + existing cmd/sonarr tests). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./cmd/sonarr/...` (cmd/sonarr 3.86s); `go vet` + gofmt clean. Remaining Arc 1 work: the J346-deferred LocalEpisode-driven file-size/mediainfo custom-format Specifications (a separate sub-arc — these are CustomFormats/Specifications, not ImportDecisionMaker specs) + the ManualImport Rejections list surface.

HEAD at `J353` — **Post-J352 residual-arc, Arc 1 (ImportDecisionMaker spec pipeline) slice 1: port the final two import Specifications — AlreadyImported + MatchesFolder — completing the 14-spec set + wiring both into production.** Orientation found that the orchestrator (`internal/episodeimport/import_decision_maker.go`) and 12 of the 14 C# `IImportDecisionEngineSpecification` classes were already ported in `specs.go`; the two unported classes were `AlreadyImportedSpecification` (C# `Specifications/AlreadyImportedSpecification.cs:11-79`) and `MatchesFolderSpecification` (C# `Specifications/MatchesFolderSpecification.cs:12-86`). Both rejection reasons (`EpisodeAlreadyImported`=18, `EpisodeUnexpected`=24) + both target subsystems (`internal/history`, `internal/parsingservice`) already existed, and `episodeimport` already imported `internal/history`, so no cycle risk. **AlreadyImportedSpecification**: new `ImportHistoryFinder` narrow interface (`FindByEpisodeId(ctx, id) []history.Entry` — lowercase-'d' spelling kept for C# grep parity, structurally satisfied by `*history.Service`); nil download item → Accept; per episode with a file (`EpisodeFileID != nil && > 0`, the Go-port `episode.HasFile` ≡ `EpisodeFileId > 0`), finds the most-recent DownloadFolderImported (3) + Grabbed (1) entries matching the item's DownloadID via the new `firstHistoryEntry` helper (history rows are date-DESC so "first" is "most recent"); rejects `EpisodeAlreadyImported` when imported-with-no-grab OR imported-after-grab; re-grabbed-after-import / never-imported → continue. **MatchesFolderSpecification**: new `FolderMatchParsingService` narrow interface (`ParseSpecialEpisodeTitle` + `GetEpisodes`, satisfied by `*parsingservice.Service`); ExistingFile → Accept; scene-season-special file/folder info re-parsed via `ParseSpecialEpisodeTitle` before episode resolution; nil file-or-folder info or empty folder-episode list → Accept; rejects `EpisodeUnexpected` for any file-episode whose ID isn't among the folder's episodes, message naming the unexpected episodes via the existing `formatEpisodeShortIDs` (`%dx%02d`) + the folder release title. Both specs translate lookup/parse errors to Accept + Warn-log (the established defensive idiom across SameEpisodes / EpisodeTitle / Upgrade — the C# tree instead lets the exception bubble to EvaluateSpec's catch → DecisionError). Both constructors return `(*Spec, error)` and reject nil deps. Both added to the `var (_ Specification = …)` compile-time guard block. Wired into `cmd/sonarr/main.go` `importDecisionSpecs` (historySvc + parsingSvc both in scope at the assembly site). 2 new test functions (`TestAlreadyImportedSpecification` 8 sub-tests + nil-finder; `TestMatchesFolderSpecification` 7 sub-tests + nil-parsing). This completes the 14-spec ImportDecisionMaker Specification set — only `UpgradeSpecification` remains ported-but-unwired (pending its ProfileView-per-batch caching layer). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/episodeimport/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` (episodeimport 1.42s, cmd/sonarr 3.69s); `go vet` + gofmt clean. Next Arc 1 sub-slices: wire UpgradeSpecification's production ProfileView-per-batch adapter; then the ManualImport Rejections list + LocalEpisode-driven file-size/mediainfo CF specs that J346 deferred (now unblocked by the complete spec set).

HEAD at `J352` — **Post-J342 service-port arc, slice 10 (Arc 3 slice 6 — DRAINS Arc 3 + COMPLETES the Post-J342 service-port arc, B-category): Queue v5 includeSubresources[] enum-array gating + Episodes-plural hydration**. The final slice — closes Arc 3 and all three service-port arcs. C# v5 `QueueController.cs:170-171` derives `includeSeries = includeSubresources.Contains(QueueSubresource.Series)` + `includeEpisodes = includeSubresources.Contains(QueueSubresource.Episodes)` from the `includeSubresources QueueSubresource[]` query param (enum {Series, Episodes}), and `QueueResource.cs:59` sets `Episodes = includeEpisodes ? model.Episodes?.ToResource() : null`. Three changes: (1) the shared `projectQueueResource` now hydrates the plural `Episodes *[]EpisodeResource` from all q.Episodes (alongside the J348 Series + singular Episode); (2) the v3 `QueueHandler` strips the plural Episodes unconditionally (v3's wire carries only the singular Episode — the plural is a v5-only field the shared adapter hydrates); (3) a new `apiv3.QueueHandlerV5` (lives in apiv3 alongside the shared QueueResource + the unexported parsePagingRequest) parses `includeSubresources` (repeated, case-insensitive "series"/"episodes"), keeps Series iff ∋series, keeps plural Episodes iff ∋episodes, and strips the singular Episode unconditionally (the v5 QueueResource dropped it). `queuev5.ListHandler` switches from the v3 alias to `apiv3.QueueHandlerV5`. The J347 scalar adapter test's Episodes-nil guard was updated to assert the plural is now hydrated. 1 new v5 handler test (`TestListHandler_IncludeSubresources_J352`, 4 sub-tests: no-subresources strips all; series-only; episodes-only keeps plural + strips singular; both). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/queue/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (v3 1.441s, v5/queue 1.802s, cmd/sonarr 3.683s); `go vet` + gofmt clean. **Post-J342 service-port arc COMPLETE** — Arc 1 (Release POST searchInfo fallback, J343) + Arc 2 (Parse + ManualImport full hydration, J344-J346) + Arc 3 (Queue paged surface fully populated v3+v5, J347-J352) all drained. The Queue paged list now serves fully-hydrated rows on both surfaces: v3 (scalar + Series/Episode-singular gated by includeSeries/includeEpisode + CustomFormats + Score + StatusMessages) and v5 (the same scalars + EpisodeIDs/SeasonNumbers/EpisodesWithFilesCount + Series/Episodes-plural gated by includeSubresources[]). The next loop tick should emit the arc-complete sentinel and stop (no further service-port arcs remain in the priority list).

HEAD at `J351` — **Post-J342 service-port arc, slice 9 (Arc 3 slice 5, B-category): Queue v5 always-set list fields — EpisodeIDs + SeasonNumbers + EpisodesWithFilesCount projection**. Projects the three ungated v5-additive QueueResource fields. C# v5 `QueueResource.cs:56-57` sets `EpisodeIds = model.Episodes?.Select(e => e.Id).ToList() ?? []` + `SeasonNumbers = model.SeasonNumber.HasValue ? [model.SeasonNumber.Value] : []` (both always), and `:81` sets `EpisodesWithFilesCount = model.Episodes?.Count(e => e.HasFile) ?? 0`. `projectQueueResource` now builds EpisodeIDs from all q.Episodes IDs (always non-nil pointer → mirrors C# `= []`; empty → wire `[]`), SeasonNumbers from q.SeasonNumber (`&[]int{*sn}` when set, else `&[]int{}`), and EpisodesWithFilesCount by counting episodes whose `EpisodeFileID != nil && > 0` (the Go-port equivalent of C# `Episode.HasFile`, which is `EpisodeFileId > 0`). These are v5-additive fields on the shared apiv3 type — v3 clients ignore the extra keys (the established J324/J318 additive-divergence posture); EpisodesWithFilesCount keeps omitempty so 0 collapses to absent (the J318 accepted trade-off). 2 new adapter tests (populated: EpisodeIDs=[11,12] + SeasonNumbers=[3] + EpisodesWithFilesCount=1 with one EpisodeFileID-bearing episode; empty: non-nil len-0 lists matching C# `= []` + count 0). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/queue/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (v3 1.674s, v5/queue 2.047s, cmd/sonarr 3.689s); `go vet` + gofmt clean. Post-J342 service-port arc: Arc 1 (J343) + Arc 2 (J344-J346) DONE; Arc 3 IN PROGRESS — scalar paged surface (J347) + Series/Episode hydration (J348) + CustomFormats/Score (J349) + StatusMessages (J350) + v5 always-set list fields (J351) landed. The ONLY remaining Arc 3 sub-slice is the v5 includeSubresources[] enum-array query-param gating + Episodes-plural subresource hydration, which needs a v5-specific QueueHandler (parses `includeSubresources=Series,Episodes` instead of aliasing the v3 includeSeries/includeEpisode handler) + the Episodes-plural projection gated on the enum. That slice closes Arc 3 → all three Post-J342 service-port arcs complete.

HEAD at `J350` — **Post-J342 service-port arc, slice 8 (Arc 3 slice 4, B-category): Queue StatusMessages projection ([]string → []QueueStatusMessage)**. Wires the J323-pinned `StatusMessages *[]QueueStatusMessage` field that was previously always nil. C# `QueueResource.cs:89` sets `StatusMessages = model.StatusMessages` (always a non-null `List<TrackedDownloadStatusMessage>` where each entry has `Title` + `Messages[]`). The Go domain simplified that to a flat `[]string` at the trackeddownloads layer (`trackeddownloads.TrackedDownload.StatusMessages []string` with a doc-comment noting "the typed status-message struct ports alongside the queue / UI integration slice that consumes it" — that's THIS slice). `projectQueueResource` now synthesises each flat string into a `apiv3.QueueStatusMessage{Title: s, Messages: []string{}}` — the natural flat-string → header-line mapping — and always sets `res.StatusMessages` to a non-nil pointer to mirror C#'s non-null list (empty domain slice → wire `"statusMessages":[]`; the queue.Queue StatusMessages is guaranteed non-nil per the queue handler's `append([]string{}, td.StatusMessages...)`). Not gated by an include flag (C# always sets it). 2 new adapter tests (populated 2-string list → 2 QueueStatusMessage entries with the strings as titles + empty Messages; empty slice → non-nil pointer to a len-0 list matching C#). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/queue/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (v3 1.549s, v5/queue 2.179s, cmd/sonarr 4.099s); `go vet` + gofmt clean. Post-J342 service-port arc: Arc 1 (J343) + Arc 2 (J344-J346) DONE; Arc 3 IN PROGRESS — scalar paged surface (J347) + Series/Episode hydration (J348) + CustomFormats/Score (J349) + StatusMessages (J350) landed. The v3 QueueResource is now FULLY populated. The only remaining Arc 3 sub-slice is the v5-specific surface: Episodes-plural + EpisodeIDs + SeasonNumbers hydration + the includeSubresources[] enum-array query-param gating (needs a v5-specific QueueHandler that parses the enum array instead of aliasing the v3 includeSeries/includeEpisode handler).

HEAD at `J349` — **Post-J342 service-port arc, slice 7 (Arc 3 slice 3, B-category): Queue CustomFormats list field + CustomFormats/CustomFormatScore projection from the row's RemoteEpisode Subject**. Discovered the Go-port `apiv3.QueueResource` was MISSING the `CustomFormats []CustomFormatResource` list field entirely (the J318 doc-comment listed it as DEFERRED — only `CustomFormatScore int` had been pinned). C# `QueueResource.cs:26` declares `List<CustomFormatResource> CustomFormats`, always set via `model.RemoteEpisode?.CustomFormats?.ToResource(false)` at `QueueResource.cs:75` + `CustomFormatScore = model.Series?.QualityProfile?.Value?.CalculateCustomFormatScore(customFormats) ?? 0`. J349 adds the missing field (`CustomFormats []CustomFormatResource json:"customFormats,omitempty"` — omitempty drops the key when no formats, matching the C# null-list-absent posture) and projects both CustomFormats + CustomFormatScore in `projectQueueResource` from the queue row's `RemoteEpisode *decisionengine.Subject` (the Subject carries the decision-time-matched CustomFormats slice + the precomputed CustomFormatScore). The Go-port reuses the Subject's precomputed score rather than recomputing against the current series QP as C# does — the two are equivalent unless the QP changed between grab and display, and reusing it avoids threading customFormatsSvc + qualityProfilesSvc lookups into the queue paged adapter (documented in the QueueResource doc-comment). CustomFormats is NOT gated by an include flag (C# always sets it). 2 new adapter tests (CustomFormats+Score projection from a populated Subject; no-RemoteEpisode → CustomFormats nil + Score 0). gofmt re-aligned the struct + adapter after the field insertion. Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/queue/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (v3 2.688s, v5/queue 2.137s, cmd/sonarr 3.831s); `go vet` + gofmt clean. Post-J342 service-port arc: Arc 1 (J343) + Arc 2 (J344-J346) DONE; Arc 3 IN PROGRESS — scalar paged surface (J347) + Series/Episode hydration (J348) + CustomFormats/Score (J349) landed; remaining Arc 3 sub-slices are StatusMessages projection (lossy []string→[]QueueStatusMessage synthesis design question) and the v5-only Episodes-plural + EpisodeIDs + SeasonNumbers hydration + includeSubresources[] enum-array query-param gating.

HEAD at `J348` — **Post-J342 service-port arc, slice 6 (Arc 3 slice 2, B-category): Queue Series + Episode (singular) subresource hydration with v3 includeSeries/includeEpisode gating**. Hydrates the two J322-pinned QueueResource subresources off the domain objects the queue.Queue row already carries (no new lookups). C# `QueueResourceMapper.ToResource(model, includeSeries, includeEpisode)` at `src/Sonarr.Api.V3/Queue/QueueResource.cs:73-74` gates `Series = includeSeries && model.Series != null ? model.Series.ToResource() : null` and `Episode = includeEpisode && model.Episode != null ? ...`; both flags default false per `QueueController.cs:141`. The Go-port uses a hydrate-then-strip posture: `projectQueueResource` (cmd/sonarr) hydrates `Series` via `apiv3.ToSeriesResource(*q.Series)` + `Episode` (singular) via `apiv3.ToEpisodeResource(q.Episodes[0])` unconditionally (keeping the QueuePagedLister interface free of wire-shape concerns), and `apiv3.QueueHandler` parses `includeSeries` + `includeEpisode` query params (default false) and strips each record's subresource back to nil when the corresponding flag is absent. v5/queue.ListHandler remains aliased to the v3 handler — v5 clients send the `includeSubresources[]` enum-array (not includeSeries/includeEpisode), so v5 currently always strips → no subresources → no regression; v5's enum-array gating is the J349 slice. The J347 adapter test's subresources-must-be-nil guard was updated to assert Series + Episode are now hydrated (Episodes plural stays nil at the adapter level). 1 new v3 handler test (`TestQueueHandler_SubresourceGating_J348`) with 3 sub-tests: no flags strips both; includeSeries=true keeps series + strips episode; both flags keep both. Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/queue/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (v3 2.105s, v5/queue 1.527s, cmd/sonarr 4.672s); `go vet` clean. Post-J342 service-port arc: Arc 1 (J343) + Arc 2 (J344-J346) DONE; Arc 3 IN PROGRESS — scalar paged surface (J347) + Series/Episode hydration (J348) landed; remaining Arc 3 sub-slices are CustomFormats/Score + StatusMessages projection, the v5-only Episodes-plural + EpisodeIDs + SeasonNumbers hydration, and the v5 includeSubresources[] enum-array query-param gating.

HEAD at `J347` — **Post-J342 service-port arc, slice 5 (Arc 3 slice 1, B-category): Queue.Service paged surface — scalar QueueResource projection + paging adapter wired to /api/v3/queue + /api/v5/queue**. Opens Arc 3 (the largest of the three arcs). Replaces the `emptyQueuePagedLister{}` stub on both queue list routes with a real `queuePagedAdapter` that reads the in-memory `queue.Service.GetQueue()` cache, paginates in cache order (matching the historyPagedAdapter convention — sorting stays DEFERRED), and projects each `queue.Queue` domain row to a scalar `apiv3.QueueResource`. C# source at `src/Sonarr.Api.V3/Queue/QueueResource.cs:58-107` (`QueueResourceMapper.ToResource(model, includeSeries, includeEpisode)`) drives the field mapping. The new `projectQueueResource(q queue.Queue) apiv3.QueueResource` helper maps the scalar field set: ID, SeriesID (from q.Series.ID), EpisodeID (from q.Episodes[0].ID — the Go domain carries Episodes[] where C# has singular model.Episode), Title, Status (CamelCaseEnumName for the JsonStringEnumConverter camelCase policy), DownloadID, Protocol (lowercase String() form), Languages (extract persisted IDs → ToLanguageRefs), Quality (ToQualityModelResource), TrackedDownloadStatus/State (CamelCaseEnumName when the pointer is non-nil), Size, SizeLeft, TimeLeft (FormatTimeLeft), Indexer, DownloadClient, DownloadClientHasPostImportCategory, OutputPath, ErrorMessage, Added, EstimatedCompletionTime. The Series / Episode / Episodes / EpisodeIDs / SeasonNumbers / StatusMessages subresources + CustomFormats + CustomFormatScore are left nil/zero — DEFERRED to the J348+ hydration slices (the queue.Queue row already carries the Series + Episodes domain objects, so hydration is projection-only — no new lookups needed). The now-dead `emptyQueuePagedLister` type was removed (CLAUDE.md dead-code policy) and the emptyQueueDetailsLister doc-comment's dangling reference fixed. 3 new tests in cmd/sonarr/queue_paged_adapter_test.go (empty queue → empty page; scalar projection correctness pinning Status="downloading"/Protocol="torrent"/TimeLeft="01:30:00"/SeriesID/EpisodeID/Languages + subresources-nil guard; pagination page-2-of-25). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./cmd/sonarr/... ./internal/api/v5/queue/... ./internal/api/v3/...` all pass (cmd/sonarr 3.841s, v5/queue 2.649s, v3 2.264s); `go vet` clean. Post-J342 service-port arc backlog: Arc 1 (J343) + Arc 2 (J344-J346) DONE; Arc 3 IN PROGRESS — scalar paged surface landed at J347; remaining Arc 3 sub-slices are Series subresource hydration, Episode/Episodes subresource hydration, CustomFormats/Score, StatusMessages projection, and the v5 includeSubresources[] enum-array query-param gating.

HEAD at `J346` — **Post-J342 service-port arc, slice 4 (Arc 2 slice 3 — DRAINS Arc 2, A-category): ManualImport reprocess handler populated CustomFormats + CustomFormatScore via the J345 calculator reused behind a new ManualImportCustomFormatCalculator optional-extension**. Closes the last Arc 2 sub-slice — the `ManualImport handler populated customFormats` row deferred at J238. The reprocess handler already parses the item path into a ParsedEpisodeInfo, resolves languages, and carries IndexerFlags + SeriesID — exactly the inputs the J345 `customformats.ParseCustomFormatForRemoteEpisode` calculator needs, so J346 reuses it rather than waiting on the full ImportDecisionMaker port. C# source at `src/Sonarr.Api.V3/ManualImport/ManualImportController.cs:62-63` sets `item.CustomFormats = processedItem.CustomFormats.ToResource(false)` + `item.CustomFormatScore = processedItem.CustomFormatScore` from `_manualImportService.ReprocessItem(...)` which runs the LocalEpisode calculator; the Go-port approximates the release-title-derivable subset (resolution / codec / release-group specs) — the file-size + mediainfo specs stay deferred because the bare-path endpoint can't synthesise those LocalEpisode fields without ImportDecisionMaker. New `apiv3.ManualImportCustomFormatCalculator interface { CalculateForManualImport(ctx, *parser.ParsedEpisodeInfo, seriesID int, languages []language.Language, indexerFlags int) ([]CustomFormatResource, int) }`. `ManualImportReprocessHandler` gains a 3rd positional optional dep `calc ManualImportCustomFormatCalculator` threaded into `reprocessOne`; after computing item.Languages, when `calc != nil && item.SeriesID > 0`, converts item.Languages (LanguageRef→language.Language via new `languagesFromLanguageRefs` helper + language.FindByID) and invokes the calculator — assigns item.CustomFormats + item.CustomFormatScore. Nil calc / no series → pre-J346 empty-slice fallback preserved. cmd/sonarr `parsingServiceWithPopulator` (J345 wrapper) gains a `CalculateForManualImport` method that resolves the series via seriesSvc, fetches allFormats, runs ParseCustomFormatForRemoteEpisode (size=0, IndexerFlags from the int param), projects + computes the score via Series.QualityProfileID. Both manualimport POST routes (v3 + v5 alias) now pass `parsingSvcWithPopulator` as the 3rd arg. v5/manualimport.ReprocessHandler is a direct `var` alias of the v3 handler so the signature change propagates automatically. 3 new v3 tests (nil-calc empty CustomFormats backwards-compat + calc-populates with typed CF + score:25 + calc-not-called-without-seriesId) + 1 new fake (fakeManualImportCalc). 18 existing v3 reprocess test call sites + 2 v5 sites updated to pass nil for the new arg via replace_all. Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/manualimport/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (v3 1.729s, v5/manualimport 2.110s, cmd/sonarr 4.022s); `go vet` clean. **Arc 2 (parsingService.Map + ImportDecisionMaker) DRAINS at J346** — Parse handler fully populated (Series/Episodes/Languages J344 + CustomFormats J345) + ManualImport CustomFormats J346. The full ImportDecisionMaker spec pipeline (Rejections list + file-size/mediainfo CF specs) remains a separate future arc, not part of the populated-mapper backlog. Post-J342 service-port arc: Arc 1 (J343) + Arc 2 (J344-J346) DONE; Arc 3 (Queue.Service paged surface + QueueResource v5 includeSubresources[] hydration) is the only remaining arc.

HEAD at `J345` — **Post-J342 service-port arc, slice 3 (Arc 2 slice 2, A-category): Parse handler populated CustomFormats + CustomFormatScore via new ParseCustomFormatForRemoteEpisode helper + ParsePopulator optional-extension type-assertion (J338/J339/J342 idiom)**. Layers the CustomFormats projection onto the J344 Map wire-through, completing the v3 + v5 Parse handler's populated-row mapper. ManualImport handler is the only Arc 2 sub-slice remaining; it's the larger port (~3 service deps + decision-maker integration). C# source at `src/Sonarr.Api.V3/Parse/ParseController.cs:53-54` calls `_formatCalculator.ParseCustomFormat(remoteEpisode, 0)` + `series.QualityProfile.Value.CalculateCustomFormatScore(remoteEpisode.CustomFormats) ?? 0` after the Map step. The Go-port equivalent: a NEW `customformats.ParseCustomFormatForRemoteEpisode(parsedInfo, ser, languages, indexerFlags, size, allFormats) []CustomFormat` helper in `internal/customformats/calculate_remoteepisode.go` that mirrors C# `CustomFormatCalculationService.ParseCustomFormat(RemoteEpisode, long)` at `:36-49`. The helper takes individual-field parameters instead of a *decisionengine.Subject pointer because the customformats package already imports nothing from decisionengine — a Subject-typed parameter would create an import cycle (decisionengine itself imports customformats for Subject.CustomFormats). Callers in cmd/sonarr + the API layer hold the Subject and pass the lifted fields. Empty allFormats / nil parsedInfo returns nil. New `apiv3.ParsePopulator interface { PopulateParseCustomFormats(ctx, *decisionengine.Subject) ([]CustomFormatResource, int) }` declares the optional-extension that wraps the calculator engine + score lookup. Both `apiv3.ParseHandler` + `parsev5.GetHandler` type-assert the mapper for `ParsePopulator` after a successful Map projection; populator non-nil → populates `CustomFormats` + `CustomFormatScore`; populator nil → CustomFormats stays nil (omitempty drops it) + Score stays 0 (matches the J344 minimal-shape deferred state verbatim). cmd/sonarr/host_wiring.go gains a NEW `parsingServiceWithPopulator` wrapper struct that embeds *parsingservice.Service (preserves the full ParseMapper surface via embedding) + holds historySeriesLookup + historyQualityProfileLookup + historyCustomFormatsLookup deps (reuses the J336 lookup interfaces for symmetry with the history + episodefile populator wrappers). Its `PopulateParseCustomFormats` method runs the C# `:36-49` sequence: fetch allFormats via customformats.Service.All; build inputs from subject.ParsedEpisodeInfo + Series + Languages + IndexerFlags-from-Release; call the new helper; project to []CustomFormatResource + compute score via qualityprofiles.CalculateCustomFormatScore against the Series.QualityProfileID. The wrapper is constructed once in v3Routes alongside historySvcPopulated + episodeFilesSvcPopulated. Both Parse routes now pass `parsingSvcWithPopulator` instead of bare `parsingSvc`. Nil mapper / nil Subject / nil Series / Map error all degrade silently to the pre-J344 minimal shape — backwards-compatible for every existing test fixture. cmd/sonarr/host_wiring.go threads `parsingSvc` to both Parse routes (`/api/v3/parse` + `/api/v5/parse`); parsingSvc was already in scope from J343. 5 new v3 tests (nil-mapper backwards compat + happy-path populated + nil-Subject fallback + nil-Series fallback + Map-error silent degradation) + 1 new v5 test (happy-path populated, exercises the v5 GetHandler signature change). HEAD at `J343` — **Post-J342 service-port arc, slice 1 (B-category): Release POST searchInfo fallback ladder ported via narrow-interface wire-through of *parsingservice.Service.GetEpisodes + *episodes.Service.GetEpisode**. Opens the new service-port arc by closing the `Release POST searchInfo fallback` row from the J342 backlog. C# `src/Sonarr.Api.V3/Indexers/ReleaseController.cs:114-153` defines the fallback ladder that fires when the cached `RemoteEpisode.Series` is nil OR `RemoteEpisode.Episodes` is empty: tries `release.EpisodeId`+single-episode lookup, falls back to `release.SeriesId`+`_parsingService.GetEpisodes(parsedInfo, series, sceneSource=true)`, else 404s with one of two distinct messages ("Unable to find matching series and episodes" when neither id supplied; "Unable to parse episodes in the release" when parsing/single-episode also fail). The v5 equivalent at `src/Sonarr.Api.V5/Release/ReleaseController.cs:135-159` is driven by the `searchInfo` body sub-block (`seriesId` + `episodeId` fields). Both Go-port surfaces were previously dispatching the cached Subject verbatim — when the cached state happened to be incomplete the dispatch would silently bypass the recovery path. Two new narrow interfaces declared in `internal/api/v3/release_write.go`: `ReleaseParsingFallback { GetEpisodes(ctx, *parser.ParsedEpisodeInfo, *series.Series, bool) ([]episodes.Episode, error) }` satisfied by *parsingservice.Service via Go structural typing; `ReleaseSingleEpisodeLookup { GetEpisode(ctx, int) (*episodes.Episode, error) }` satisfied by *episodes.Service. ReleaseDownloadHandler signature extended with these 2 new positional deps after the existing 4 — both nil-tolerant (well-formed cached Subject fast-paths regardless; broken Subject + nil deps surfaces as 503 ServiceUnavailable matching the override-branch pattern at J249). New `applyParsingFallback(ctx, body, decision, seriesLookup, singleEpLookup, parsingFallback) (Decision, int, string)` helper ports the 4 fallback arms verbatim from C#: Subject.Series==nil + body.EpisodeId>0 → single-episode lookup + series-by-episode-SeriesID lookup; Subject.Series==nil + body.SeriesId>0 → series lookup + parsing fallback with sceneSource=true (404 on empty result with the C# `:130` message); Subject.Series==nil + neither id → 404 with the C# `:138` message; Subject.Series!=nil + Episodes empty → parsing fallback + optional single-episode fallback when parsing returns empty and body.EpisodeId>0; final guard → 404 with the C# `:155` message. The cloned Subject preserves Release / SceneMapping / MappedSeasonNumber / CustomFormats / etc. via shallow struct copy (matches the J249 override-clone pattern at C# `:93-104`) so the cache stays pristine — cache invariant pin in `TestReleaseDownloadHandler_Fallback_CachedSubjectNotMutated`. v5/release/release.go `DownloadHandler` signature extended with the same 2 deps + threads them to the v3 handler; v5 body translation gains `body.SearchInfo.SeriesID/EpisodeID → v3Body.SeriesID/EpisodeID` so the v3 ladder fires against the v5 wire shape (DEFERRED comments on the v5 file dropped). cmd/sonarr `buildHostServer` + `v3Routes` signatures grew a `parsingSvc *parsingservice.Service` parameter; production wire sites pass `parsingSvc, episodesSvc` (episodesSvc satisfies both `ReleaseParsingFallback` via *parsingservice.Service and `ReleaseSingleEpisodeLookup` via the Service.GetEpisode method directly). 8 new tests covering all 4 fallback arms + the 503 nil-deps case + the cache-pristine invariant in v3; 1 new test (`TestDownloadHandler_SearchInfo_SeriesIDRoutesToV3Fallback`) in v5/release pinning the searchInfo wire-through. 2 existing tests (CacheHitDispatches in v3 + CacheHit_EchoesV5BodyAndDispatches in v5) updated to fixture well-formed Subjects (Series + Episodes populated) — previously those tests would have failed the C# fallback even pre-J343 if exercised at the controller level; the pre-J343 Go-port silently bypassed the recovery. Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/release/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (v3 1.7s, v5/release 2.4s, cmd/sonarr 4.0s); `go vet ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/release/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` clean. Post-J342 service-port arc backlog: 1 of 3 sub-arcs landed (Release POST searchInfo fallback drain DONE); remaining sub-arcs are parsingService.Map + ImportDecisionMaker → Parse + ManualImport populated customFormats (A-cat) and Queue.Service paged surface → QueueResource v5 includeSubresources[] hydration (B-cat).

Doc comment on HistoryResource extended with a J312 paragraph cataloguing each field's wire contract + the C# population path + the deferral rationale. 2 new tests in `history_test.go`: `TestHistorySinceHandler_EmitsQualityCutoffNotMetAndSubresources_J312` — 2-row pin covering BOTH deferred-state branches: both rows leave QualityCutoffNotMet at the zero-value default (positive assertion `strings.Count(body, "\"qualityCutoffNotMet\":false") == 2` — pinning the always-emit contract for the non-nullable bool); both rows have nil Episode + nil Series (negative pins `!strings.Contains(body, "\"episode\":")` + `!strings.Contains(body, "\"series\":")` — pinning the omitempty drop for the pointer-to-struct subresources). `TestHistoryResource_PopulatedSubresources_EmitFullShape_J312` — pure-marshal pin for the populated-subresource branch (no service path today populates these; the test directly constructs a HistoryResource with `&EpisodeResource{ID:101, EpisodeNumber:1, SeasonNumber:1, Title:"Pilot"}` + `&SeriesResource{ID:11, Title:"Show", Tags:[]int{}, Genres:[]string{}}` + flipped `QualityCutoffNotMet=true`; substring asserts on all 7 must-have keys including the populated `"episode":{`/`"series":{` openers + nested `"title":"Pilot"`/`"title":"Show"` confirmations). Existing positive-pin tests (`TestHistorySinceHandler_EmitsCustomFormatsAndScore`, `TestHistorySinceHandler_EmitsDataDictAndOmitsWhenNil`) continue to pass — their rows have nil Episode + nil Series (omitempty drops the keys) and zero-value QualityCutoffNotMet (emits `:false` which the existing assertions don't check for). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v3/... ./cmd/sonarr/... ./internal/api/v5/history/...` all pass (v3 1.407s, cmd/sonarr 3.679s, v5/history 1.993s). Post-Wave-3 drain backlog: 4 of B-category items closed (J309 SeasonResource Stats+Images; J310 HistoryResource data; J311 BlocklistResource 4-field scalar; J312 HistoryResource QualityCutoffNotMet + Episode? + Series?). Combined: 11 wire-shape fields landed across the History/Blocklist/SeasonResource family. Remaining: Quality/Languages typed DTOs (each its own slice — Quality is a complex envelope, Languages is a typed list with id-name lookup), BlocklistResource Series subresource (same J312-pattern but bigger because BlocklistResource also wants Languages/Quality), Source v5-only field, SeriesResource v5 divergences (needs alias break), QueueResource includeSubresources hydration, Release POST searchInfo fallback.

New exported `apiv3.BlocklistProtocolName(*int) string` helper centralises the `*int → DownloadProtocol → camelCase string` projection (nil → "unknown" matches C# enum default-zero name); placed in `internal/api/v3/blocklist.go` so future paged consumers (history + queue) can reuse without duplicating. Imports new `internal/indexers` dep (already a project package providing the `DownloadProtocol` enum + `.String()` method). Production paged consumer at `cmd/sonarr/host_wiring.go:blocklistPagedAdapter.PagedBlocklist` populates all four fields + EpisodeIDs nil-fallback to `[]int{}`. v5/blocklist aliases v3 via `type Resource = apiv3.BlocklistResource` so the widening propagates to both surfaces simultaneously. Existing positive-pin test `TestBlocklistHandler_EmitsCustomFormatsField` continues to pass — its row has nil EpisodeIDs + empty Indexer/Message + empty Protocol (the default zero-value string), so the new fields' omitempty drops + protocol-default-emit posture still applies. HEAD at `J309` — **Post-Wave-3 drain, slice 1: v5 SeasonResource Statistics + Images pointer-field widening (J302 pattern)**. The Wave 3 arc DRAINED at J308; this slice opens the post-Wave-3 backlog by closing the smallest queued struct-pointer widening — the J307 follow-up explicitly cited: "Statistics + Images intentionally omitted from the struct today — adding them later as `*Statistics` + `*[]MediaCover` pointer-to-slice (J302 pattern) preserves the WhenWritingNull wire posture without breaking this single-monitor endpoint." `internal/api/v5/series/series.go` extends `SeasonResource` from 2 declared fields → 4: adds `Statistics *SeasonStatistics` + `Images *[]MediaCover` both with `json:",omitempty"`. New `SeasonStatistics` struct (11 fields) mirrors `src/Sonarr.Api.V5/Series/SeasonStatisticsResource.cs:7-21` — v5 adds `MonitoredEpisodeCount`, `ReleaseTypes`, `EpisodeFileQualities` over the v3 shape. Non-nullable scalars (EpisodeFileCount, EpisodeCount, TotalEpisodeCount, MonitoredEpisodeCount, SizeOnDisk, PercentOfEpisodes) always emit when the parent Statistics pointer is non-nil; nullable slices (`ReleaseGroups`, `ReleaseTypes`, `EpisodeFileQualities`) use omitempty so nil drops the key (matches `JsonIgnoreCondition.WhenWritingNull`). `ReleaseTypes` is `[]string` carrying the camelCase enum strings ("singleEpisode"/"multiEpisode"/"seasonPack"/"unknown") that STJson's global `JsonStringEnumConverter(CamelCase)` produces. `EpisodeFileQualities` is `[]json.RawMessage` — the Quality DTO is complex enough that a typed projection belongs to the populated-mapper port arc, not the wire-pin slice. `PercentOfEpisodes` is a stored float64 — C# computes it (returns 0 when EpisodeCount=0); the Go-port leaves the computation to whichever populated-mapper allocates the struct. New `MediaCover` struct (CoverType string + Url string + RemoteUrl string with omitempty) is v5-local — the existing `mediacover.Cover` keeps `CoverType` as a typed int (the Series.Images JSON blob persists the legacy int representation), while v5's STJson wires the enum as camelCase string. The two types stay decoupled: the populated-mapper translates `mediacover.Cover.CoverType.String()` → this struct's `CoverType` string field at projection time. **No production caller populates these fields today** — the SeasonMonitorHandler still emits `SeasonResource{SeasonNumber, Monitored}` so both pointers stay nil and omitempty drops the keys; the existing positive-pin test (`TestSeasonMonitorHandler_Success_Returns200WithBodyAndUpdatesSeasonsJSON`) continues to pass with its `must NOT contain "images"`/`must NOT contain "statistics"` negative assertions. 4 new wire-shape tests in `series_test.go`: `TestSeasonResource_NilStatisticsAndImages_OmitKeys` (canonical nil-pointer → key-absent pin); `TestSeasonResource_PopulatedStatistics_EmitsFullNestedShape` (pinned `2026-01-02T03:04:05Z` date + 11-field nested shape with mixed nil-and-populated child fields — covers PreviousAiring + EpisodeFileQualities nil-omitempty within the populated parent); `TestSeasonResource_EmptyImagesSlice_EmitsEmptyArray` (`&[]MediaCover{}` → `"images":[]` — the 3-state pointer-to-slice contract that distinguishes "explicitly empty" from "absent"); `TestSeasonResource_PopulatedImages_EmitsCoverEntriesWithStringCoverType` (CoverType string-enum pin + RemoteUrl-omitempty pin via `strings.Count == 1` defensive assertion). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go vet ./internal/api/v5/series/... ./cmd/sonarr/...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v5/series/... ./internal/api/v3/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (v5/series 1.637s — 16 tests now: 3 CRUD-DELETE + 4 Editor + 5 SeasonMonitor + 4 wire-shape pin; v3 1.581s; cmd/sonarr 3.692s). Wire-shape pin only; populated allocation arrives with the post-Wave-3 SeriesStatistics service-port arc.

HEAD at `J308` — **Wave 3 LARGE-family arc DRAINED**: Release family final residual ported — v5-NEW POST /api/v5/release with ReleaseGrabResource body shape (343 LOC C# — `ReleaseController.cs:86-188`). New v5-specific Go types in `v5/release` package: `GrabResource` (4 fields: `guid`, `indexerId`, `override?`, `searchInfo?`) + `OverrideResource` (5 fields: `seriesId?`, `episodeIds`, `downloadClientId?`, `quality?`, `languages?` — all but `episodeIds` are pointers/RawMessage to mirror C# nullable optionality) + `SearchInfoResource` (3 nullable int fields). Mirrors `ReleaseGrabResource.cs:6-12`. `DownloadHandler(cache, dispatcher, seriesLookup, episodeLookup)` is a **body-translation adapter** — decodes the v5 body via `DisallowUnknownFields`, validates Guid+IndexerId at the v5 surface, translates the v5 shape to a v3 `apiv3.ReleaseDownloadBody` (guid/indexerId direct; `override` populated → `ShouldOverride=true` + flattens override.SeriesID/EpisodeIDs/DownloadClientID/Quality/Languages onto the v3 root), re-encodes as JSON, calls the underlying `apiv3.ReleaseDownloadHandler` against a `v5util.BufferingWriter` to capture the v3 result, then either echoes the v5 body back verbatim on 200 success (matches C# `return TypedResults.Ok(release)` at ReleaseController.cs:187) or passes the v3 error envelope through unchanged. v5 `searchInfo` fallback branch (C# lines 133-159 — populated branch lookup when cached `remoteEpisode.Series == null`) DEFERRED: today's cached Decision always carries populated Series + Episodes via the decision-engine pipeline so the fallback is unreachable; field decoded for wire-shape parity. 7 new tests in `release_test.go`: `TestDownloadHandler_CacheMissReturns404` (passthrough — v3 cache-miss surface preserved); `TestDownloadHandler_CacheHit_EchoesV5BodyAndDispatches` (positive comprehensive pin: 200 + v5 body echoed + dispatcher fired once + NEGATIVE pins on `shouldOverride`/`episodeIds` to confirm v3-flat-shape fields don't leak through into the v5 response); `TestDownloadHandler_ValidatorGuidRequired_PassesThrough400` + `TestDownloadHandler_ValidatorIndexerIDRequired_PassesThrough400` (v3 validation envelope passthrough); `TestDownloadHandler_MalformedBody_400`; `TestDownloadHandler_UnknownField_RejectedAt400` (defends the v5 wire contract against client confusion — a `shouldOverride` field at the root is rejected because DisallowUnknownFields trips before translation); `TestDownloadHandler_OverrideRequiredFieldsValidated_400` (incomplete override → 400 via the v3 applyShouldOverride validation chain — empty episodeIds[] + null quality/languages). HEAD at `J307` — Wave 3 LARGE-family v5 endpoint arc, **Series family final residual: v5-NEW PUT /api/v5/series/{id}/season** (per-id season-monitor endpoint at `SeriesController.cs:223-246`; no v3 equivalent — genuinely new code path). New `SeasonResource` type in v5/series package (2-field declared struct: `seasonNumber` + `monitored`; `Statistics` + `Images` DEFERRED — pointer-to-slice/struct fields can be added later per the J302 pattern without breaking the wire shape this slice pins). New `SeasonMonitorHandler(svc Writer, emitter Emitter)` decodes the v5 body, loads the series via `svc.GetSeries` (404 on miss), walks the persisted `Seasons` JSON blob via the `[]map[string]any` decode/re-encode preservation contract (matches J214 `updateSeasonsJSON` pattern at `apiv3/seasonpass.go:215-252` — per-season `Statistics` + `Images` + any other unknown fields round-trip unchanged via the opaque map representation), finds the entry whose `seasonNumber` matches the body's `seasonNumber` (404 on season-miss — matches C# `series.Seasons.FirstOrDefault(...) == null`), overwrites only the `monitored` key on that entry, re-encodes the array, persists via repo-layer `svc.Update` (matches the established v3 SeriesEditor + SeasonPass pattern — service-layer cascade DEFERRED per the 5.D1a contract), emits the SignalR `series/updated` broadcast with the freshly-updated full `apiv3.ToSeriesResource` (matches C# `BroadcastResourceChange(ModelAction.Updated, GetSeriesResource(series, false))` at line 242), returns `200 + {seasonNumber, monitored}` ONLY (matches C# `season.ToResource()` default `includeImages: false`). New helper `setSeasonMonitoredJSON` is the single-season-overlay primitive; empty/whitespace Seasons JSON short-circuits to `(existing, false, nil)` so the endpoint surfaces 404 on the fresh-imported-series-with-no-seasons edge case (matches the C# `FirstOrDefault` miss). 5 new tests in `series_test.go`: `TestSeasonMonitorHandler_Success_Returns200WithBodyAndUpdatesSeasonsJSON` (positive pin: 200 + 2-field body + persisted JSON has `monitored:false` on season 2 + the other 2 seasons preserved + `images[]` survives round-trip + SignalR Updated broadcast fires with series id=11); `TestSeasonMonitorHandler_SeriesNotFound_404` (series-miss → 404 + SignalR emit-suppression); `TestSeasonMonitorHandler_SeasonNumberNotFound_404` (season-miss → 404 + persisted JSON UNCHANGED — no half-mutate); `TestSeasonMonitorHandler_MalformedBody_400`; `TestSeasonMonitorHandler_InvalidID_400`. `fakeEmitter` extended to record `updated` broadcasts. One new v5 route wired in `host_wiring.go` v5Routes() immediately after the J304 SeriesEditor block, with a J307 prefix comment cataloguing the new wire shape + JSON preservation + SignalR posture. Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go vet ./internal/api/v5/series/... ./cmd/sonarr/...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v5/series/... ./internal/api/v3/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (v5/series 1.5s — 12 tests now: 3 CRUD-DELETE + 4 Editor + 5 SeasonMonitor; v3 1.4s; cmd/sonarr 3.6s). Series family fully complete at v5 surface. Sole residual on the entire Wave 3 LARGE-family arc: **Release POST** (v5-NEW body-shape divergence — `ReleaseGrabResource` decode + translate to v3 dispatcher).

Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go vet ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/customformat/...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v3/... ./internal/api/v5/customformat/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (v3 1.7s, v5/customformat 2.3s, cmd/sonarr 4.3s). HEAD at `J295` — Wave 1 custom-format calculator arc, **final slice: J273 LogResource `method` field omission on v5**. The calculator-engine sub-slices 1-4 of the loop scope (spec evaluator port, ParsedEpisodeInfo→CustomFormatInput projection, Calculate() service, qualityprofiles.CalculateCustomFormatScore adapter) all landed pre-J251 — sub-slices 5 (handler wire-through) and 6 (LogResource method omission) were the two outstanding items. This slice drains item 6. v5's `LogResource` per `src/Sonarr.Api.V5/Logs/LogResource.cs` carries 7 fields (id/time/exception?/exceptionType?/level/logger/message) where v3's carries 8 (adds `method?`). The J273 path-alias deferred the field-divergence — invisible at empty-corpus because v3 LogHandler returns `records:[]`. This slice replaces the `type Resource = apiv3.LogResource` alias with a v5-specific 7-field struct (no Method) AND wraps the v3 LogHandler with a `stripMethod` middleware that JSON-decodes the paged envelope, removes the `method` key from each record under `records[]`, and re-emits. Strip is a no-op at empty-corpus (records `[]`); when the log DB plumbing lands and v3 starts emitting populated records with `method`, the v5 wire stays 7-field clean without further changes. Non-200 status / non-object body / no-records-key bodies all pass through verbatim — failure-path envelopes (400/500) preserved. New `stripMethod` helper kept inline for the first consumer (J283 first-consumer rule — same posture as the GeneralSettings stripField helper); consolidates into v5util only if a second paged-record-field-strip need appears. v5 LogHandler changes from a `var = apiv3.LogHandler` alias to a `func LogHandler() http.Handler { return stripMethod(apiv3.LogHandler()) }` wrapper. host_wiring.go already calls `logv5.LogHandler()` so no route changes needed. Test file gains 7 new tests: Resource-omits-method-field (reflect-based pin), Resource-json-omits-method-key, stripMethod-removes-method-from-each-record (canonical populated-records pin via 2-row fake upstream), stripMethod-empty-records-noop, stripMethod-non-200-passes-through (500 + method-bearing error envelope preserved), stripMethod-non-object-body-passes-through (`null` body), stripMethod-no-records-key-passes-through (envelope-level `method` preserved when records[] absent). Removed: 2 type-alias tests (Resource/v3.LogResource roundtrip — no longer valid since Resource is a distinct struct). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go vet ./internal/api/v5/log/... ./cmd/sonarr/...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v5/log/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (1.7s + 3.7s). Wave 1 custom-format calculator arc DRAINED — sub-slices 1-4 pre-existed, sub-slice 5 wire-through is broader cross-cutting work tracked separately (per-handler scope across Parse/Update/Blocklist/Wanted/Calendar/History/ManualImport), sub-slice 6 lands here.

HEAD at `J294` — Wave 3 forty-third slice: v5 **ReleasePush** family port (120 LOC C# — ReleasePushController only). 2 endpoints: POST /release/push (push a release for evaluation + dispatch) + GET /release/push/{id} (inherited RestController, no-op since pushes aren't persisted). POST /release/push is a pure path-alias to v3's ReleasePushHandler — same 200+ReleaseResource shape on success; 400+envelope on validation failure (Title/DownloadUrl-or-MagnetUrl/Protocol/PublishDate per C# PostValidator). The ReleaseResource projection inherits the J237 deferred chain — current v3 Go-port emits a stub projection (full ReleaseResource widening pending). v5 inherits the same. Same J266/J270/J274/J292 "v3-port-has-no-handler gap → v5 ByID stub" pattern. 400 on invalid id (non-positive int). SignalR: ReleasePushController does NOT declare any IHandle<> interfaces — push operations don't broadcast their own events (the underlying dispatcher's grab event flows through the existing command-bus subscription, carries through unchanged on the v5 side via the shared emitter channel). New `internal/api/v5/releasepush/` package (~85 LOC source + ~85 LOC test): 5 type aliases (PushBody = apiv3.ReleasePushBody, DecisionMaker = apiv3.ReleaseDecisionMaker, Dispatcher = apiv3.ReleaseDispatcher, IndexerLookup = apiv3.ReleasePushIndexerLookup, DownloadClientLookup = apiv3.ReleasePushDownloadClientLookup) + 1 variable alias (PostHandler = apiv3.ReleasePushHandler) + 1 new v5-specific handler (ByIDHandler — always 404). 5 tests: ByIDHandler-always-404 (canonical no-lookup pin), ByIDHandler-any-valid-id-404 (id=12345 still 404), ByIDHandler-invalid-id-400 (non-numeric), ByIDHandler-negative-id-400 (non-positive rejected), PostHandlerAlias-handler-type (compile-time pin via blank-identifier reference), PushBody-type-alias (Title field addressable). 2 new routes wired alongside the existing /api/v3/release/push route in cmd/sonarr/host_wiring.go (re-uses downloadDecider + processDownloadDecisions + releaseCache + indexersRepo + downloadClientsRepo deps verbatim — same 5-arg constructor signature as v3). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go vet`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v5/releasepush/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (1.4s + 3.7s). v5 surface coverage 46/~50 → 47/~50. **Wave 3 small-tier DRAINED** — all v5 endpoint families ≤120 LOC ported. Validation chain: v5 C# adds ImportListExclusionExistsValidator (per controller :24-26) checking tvdbId uniqueness server-side before insert; v3 Go-port handles same uniqueness check at the service layer (insert returns ErrAlreadyExists → handler maps to 409). HEAD at `J283` — Wave 3 thirty-second slice: v5 **GeneralSettings** family port (207 LOC C# across GeneralSettingsController + GeneralSettingsResource; uses shared SettingsController<T> base). 3 endpoints: GET /api/v5/settings/general, GET /api/v5/settings/general/{id}, PUT /api/v5/settings/general/{id}. ONE wire-shape divergence from v3 HostConfigController: v5 OMITS the `trustCgnatIpAddresses` field (newer field added to v3 only — post-v5-fork). Other 37 fields are present on both v3 and v5 with byte-identical JSON tag spelling and types. Status codes match v3 directly: GET → 200, PUT → 202 Accepted. Strategy: wrap v3's HostConfigHandler + HostConfigUpdateHandler with a field-strip middleware (new private `stripField` helper in the v5 generalsettings package) that buffers the v3 response, JSON-decodes the body, deletes the trustCgnatIpAddresses key, and re-marshals before forwarding. v3's DisallowUnknownFields decoder accepts v5-shaped POST bodies because the only missing field defaults to `false` (zero value) — indistinguishable from an explicit `trustCgnatIpAddresses:false` send. Error bodies (400/404/500), non-JSON responses, and non-success status codes all pass through unchanged. The C# v5 controller adds a user-upsert SaveSettings override (when Username+Password both set, _userService.Upsert called before base SaveSettings) which v3 Go-port doesn't carry — DEFERRED on the v5 Go-port surface as well; pending an IUserService Go port (tracked as v5 follow-up gap). Validation chain in C# v5 is substantially richer than v3 Go-port (FileExistsValidator + IsValidCertificate chain on SslCertPath when EnableSsl, FileExistsValidator on SslKeyPath, PasswordConfirmation match-against-stored, AuthenticationMethod NotEqual(Basic) with custom error message) — same DEFERRED state as the J282 MediaManagement deep validators, pending the disk-validator + IUserService adapter shims. HEAD at `J275` — Wave 3 twenty-fourth slice: v5 **SystemTask** family port (79 LOC C# across 2 files — TaskController + TaskResource at src/Sonarr.Api.V5/System/Tasks/). 2 endpoints (GET list + GET by-id), both 200. ONE wire-shape divergence from v3 Go-port: v5 emits an additional `lastDuration` field (TimeSpan as string, computed as `LastExecution - LastStartTime` per TaskController.cs:56) which v3 Go-port omits entirely (J231 redacted the timing-derived field from the v3 corpus rather than emit it; v3 C# carries it as a computed property at TaskResource.cs:15 but Go-port chose absence). v5 surface MUST include it to match openapi schema. v5 wire shape: 8 fields total (id/name/taskName/interval/lastExecution/lastStartTime/nextExecution/lastDuration). `lastStartTime` shape difference: v5 openapi declares it non-nullable (`type: string, format: date-time`) whereas v3 Go-port uses `*time.Time` with `omitempty` — v5 emits unconditionally; when underlying Task.LastStartTime is nil, surface as zero `time.Time{}` which JSON-marshals to `"0001-01-01T00:00:00Z"` matching .NET default(DateTime) parity. `lastDuration` format: .NET TimeSpan default uses ToString("c") layout `[-][d.]hh:mm:ss[.fffffff]` — zero is `"00:00:00"`, five minutes is `"00:05:00"`, 1.5s is `"00:00:01.5000000"`. New `formatTimeSpan()` helper implements the canonical .NET TimeSpan rendering (handles days prefix + 7-digit ticks fractional). Two wire-shape divergences from v3 + 1 v3-port-gap closure: (1) SystemResource omits v3's `isContainerized` field AND adds 3 deployment-info fields (`packageVersion`, `packageAuthor`, `packageUpdateMechanismMessage`) — v3 Go-port doesn't wire IDeploymentInfoProvider, so the 3 added fields emit as empty strings at empty-corpus (Wave 1 deferred-fields chain widens both v3 and v5 once the provider lands). v5 wire shape: 31 fields total (28 v3-shared + 3 new) minus isContainerized = 31; v3 wire is 29 fields. (2) GET /system/routes/duplicate emits `{}` (object) per the v5 openapi schema (`type: object, additionalProperties: array<string>` — Dictionary<string,List<string>> at C#). v3 Go-port chose `[]` per an earlier design call — left untouched to avoid v3-side regression risk. (3) v3-port-gap closure: GET /system/routes is a v3-port gap (neither v3 nor v5 Go-port has a DfaGraphWriter equivalent) — v5 stub emits empty `text/plain; charset=utf-8` body, J266 pattern (close the gap on the v5 wire only). When a route-graph dumper lands, both v3 and v5 widen via a shared adapter. POST /system/shutdown + /system/restart are pure path-aliases via `apiv3.SystemShutdownHandler` / `apiv3.SystemRestartHandler` — identical wire shape (200 + `{shuttingDown:true}` or `{restarting:true}`, async lifecycle dispatch). New `internal/api/v5/system/` package (175 LOC source + 197 LOC test): v5-specific StatusContext (embeds apiv3.SystemStatusContext + 3 new string fields) + StatusGetter type alias to apiv3.SystemStatusGetter (no new dynamic fields) + v5-specific Resource struct (31 fields, no isContainerized) + StatusHandler + LifecycleRunner type alias + ShutdownHandler / RestartHandler variable aliases + 2 new handler funcs (RoutesDuplicateHandler emits `{}`; RoutesHandler emits empty text/plain). 7 tests: StatusHandler-v5-shape-omits-isContainerized (decodes JSON into map and asserts absence; positive-asserts presence of packageVersion + packageAuthor + packageUpdateMechanismMessage + packageUpdateMechanism; pins authentication / packageUpdateMechanism camelCase), RoutesDuplicateHandler-emits-empty-object (pins `{}` not `[]`), RoutesHandler-emits-empty-text-plain (pins Content-Type + empty body), ShutdownHandlerAlias-responds-and-dispatches (mutex-protected fakeLifecycle), RestartHandlerAlias-responds-and-dispatches, StatusContext-V3-embedding (compile-time pin on the extensible struct shape). 5 new routes wired alongside the existing /api/v3/system/* routes in cmd/sonarr/host_wiring.go (re-uses systemStatus + cfg + lifecycleSvc deps that already back v3 — StatusContext constructed inline via `systemv5.StatusContext{V3: systemStatus}` leaving Package* fields as zero strings). Hard gates clean: `go build ./...`, `go vet ./internal/api/v5/system/... ./cmd/sonarr/...`, `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/api/v5/system/... ./cmd/sonarr/...` all pass (7 v5/system tests + cmd/sonarr suite green, 1.4s + 3.7s respectively). v5 surface coverage 26/~50 → 27/~50. Next-tier remaining: System/Tasks (65 LOC), System/Backup (135 LOC), Profiles/Quality + Release (88+28+95 = 211 LOC across 2 sub-families), Settings/* (multiple controllers), Episodes (85 LOC for EpisodeController alone).

HEAD at `J273` — Wave 3 twenty-second slice: v5 **Logs** family port (286 LOC C# across 6 files — LogController + LogResource + LogFileController + LogFileControllerBase + LogFileResource + UpdateLogFileController). 5 endpoints (all GET, all 200). Pure path-alias slice (J251/J259/J264/J268/J271 pattern) — v3 Go-port already exposes all 5. LogResource v3 carries 8 fields (id, time, exception?, exceptionType?, level, logger, message, **method?**) vs v5's 6 fields (omits `method`) — invisible at empty-corpus because v3 returns `records: []` (log DB plumbing not ported; same DEFERRED state per `internal/api/v3/log.go:8-12`). When log-DB lands, v5 will need a v5-specific Resource that omits `method` — noted. LogFileResource IDENTICAL 5-field shape between v3 and v5 (id, filename, lastWriteTime, contentsUrl, downloadUrl). Both versions reuse LogFileControllerBase pattern so per-file projection is byte-equal. SortKey rewrite (`id` → `time` on response) preserved via v3 handler — matches C# v5 LogController.cs:73 behaviour. `level` query-param cascade filter (fatal → error → warn → info → debug → trace) implemented in v5 but ignored by v3 stub — invisible at empty-corpus, same chain. New `internal/api/v5/log/` package: 4 type aliases (Resource = apiv3.LogResource, FileResource, FileLister, UpdateFileLister) + 5 variable aliases (LogHandler, FileListHandler, FileByNameHandler, UpdateFileListHandler, UpdateFileByNameHandler). NO new handler code — pure re-export. 7 tests: Log-empty-corpus-200-paged-empty (pins `records:[]` + sortKey-rewritten-to-time), FileList-nonexistent-folder-empty-array, FileByName-missing-404, FileByName-invalid-filename-400 (path-traversal regex guard), UpdateFileList-nonexistent-folder-empty-array, variable-aliases non-nil, type-aliases compile-time pin. 5 new routes wired alongside existing /api/v3/log routes (re-uses logFileSvc which satisfies both FileLister and UpdateFileLister via duck-typing). Hard gates clean. v5 surface coverage 25/~50 → 26/~50. Drains the queued ascending Wave 3 list — remaining families (Series, EpisodeFiles, Queue, ImportList, AutoTagging, etc.) are deferred per loop contract.

HEAD at `J272` — Wave 3 twenty-first slice: v5 **Commands** family port (247 LOC C# across 2 files — CommandController + CommandResource). 4 endpoints. Resource shape IDENTICAL field-for-field to v3 CommandResource (id, name, commandName, message, body polymorphic payload, priority, status, result, queued, started, ended, duration, exception, trigger, clientUserAgent, stateChangeTime, sendUpdatesToClient, updateScheduledTask, lastExecutionTime — 19 wire fields; C# uses computed properties for stateChangeTime/sendUpdatesToClient/updateScheduledTask while v3 Go-port mapper hand-rolls the same precedence; `completionMessage` is `[JsonIgnore]` so never on the wire). HEAD at `J267` — Wave 3 sixteenth slice: v5 **Connections** family port (154 LOC C# across 3 files — ConnectionController + ConnectionResource + ConnectionBulkResource). 9 endpoints. Other 8 endpoints (GET list, GET /{id}, GET /schema, POST /, PUT /{id}, POST /testall, POST /action/{name}, GET /indexerflag) are pure path-aliases — same wire shape AND same status AND same path.
